Chohan,Jordi Temprana-Salvador,Enrico Munari,Luca Cima,Giulia Querzoli,Yosamin Gonzalez Belisario,Jaeike W. Faber,Geert J. L. H. van Leenders,Jan H. von der Thüsen,Lodewijk A. A. Brosens,Ronald R. de Krijger,Pieter Wesseling,Sandrine Florquin,Mateusz Maniewski,Adam Kowalewski,Robert Barna,Dina Tiniakos,Joan Lop Gros,Rogier Donders,Jake S. F. Maurits,Ming Yang Lu,Chengkuan Chen,Faisal Mahmood,Jeroen van der Laak,Nadieh Khalili,Frédérique Meeuwsen,Francesco Ciompi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DALPHIN是第一个用于病理学AI助手的多中心开放基准测试，包含来自300个病例的1236张图像，覆盖130种诊断和14个亚专科。对三种AI助手（GPT-5、Gemini 2.5 Pro、PathChat+）和31位病理学家的对比表明，PathChat+在6项任务中有4项达到专家级水平，DALPHIN已公开释放。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 数字病理学领域涌现出具有视觉问答能力的基础模型，但缺乏独立、多中心的基准测试来评估这些AI助手在常规诊断中辅助病理学家的潜力。

Method: 构建了DALPHIN基准测试，包含来自300个病例的1236张图像，涵盖130种常见至罕见诊断、6个国家、14个亚专科。设计了顺序回答和独立回答两种生成模式，对两个通用模型（GPT-5、Gemini 2.5 Pro）和一个病理专用模型（PathChat+）进行评估，并与来自10个国家的31位病理学家的表现进行对比。

Result: PathChat+在6项任务中有4项未观察到与专家水平有统计学显著差异，Gemini在2/6任务中达到该水平，GPT在1/6任务中达到。DALPHIN采用隔离、间接访问的真实标签，确保基准测试的鲁棒性和持久性。

Conclusion: DALPHIN是首个用于病理学AI助手的多中心开放基准测试，评估了当前最先进的AI模型，结果表明病理专用模型PathChat+在多项诊断任务中接近专家水平，但不同模型间存在显著差距。数据集、方法和评估平台已公开。

Original Abstract: Foundation models with visual question answering capabilities for digital pathology are emerging. Such unprecedented technology requires independent benchmarking to assess its potential in assisting pathologists in routine diagnostics. We created DALPHIN, the first multicentric open benchmark for pathology AI copilots, comprising 1236 images from 300 cases, spanning 130 rare to common diagnoses, 6 countries, and 14 subspecialties. The DALPHIN design and dataset are introduced alongside a human performance benchmark of 31 pathologists from 10 countries with varying expertise. We report results for two general-purpose (GPT-5, Gemini 2.5 Pro) and one pathology-specific copilot (PathChat+) for sequential and independent answer generation. We observed no statistically significant difference from expert-level performance in four of six tasks for PathChat, 2/6 tasks for Gemini, and 1/6 tasks for GPT. DALPHIN is publicly released with sequestered, indirectly accessible ground truth to foster robust and enduring benchmarking. Data, methods, and the evaluation platform are accessible through dalphin.grand-challenge.org.

中文摘要翻译: 具备视觉问答能力的数字病理学基础模型正在兴起。这一前所未有的技术需要独立的基准测试来评估其在辅助病理学家进行常规诊断方面的潜力。我们创建了DALPHIN——首个用于病理学AI助手的多中心开放基准测试，包含来自300个病例的1236张图像，涵盖130种常见至罕见诊断、6个国家和14个亚专科。DALPHIN的设计和数据集与来自10个国家、不同专业水平的31位病理学家的人类表现基准一同发布。我们报告了两种通用型（GPT-5、Gemini 2.5 Pro）和一种病理专用型（PathChat+）AI助手在顺序回答和独立回答生成模式下的结果。PathChat在6项任务中的4项、Gemini在6项任务中的2项、GPT在6项任务中的1项未观察到与专家水平存在统计学显著差异。DALPHIN以公开方式发布，采用隔离的、间接访问的真实标签，以促进稳健且持久的基准测试。数据、方法和评估平台可通过 dalphin.grand-challenge.org 访问。

</details>


### [31] [Erase Persona, Forget Lore: Benchmarking Multimodal Copyright Unlearning in Large Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03547)
*JuneHyoung Kwon,JungMin Yun,YoungBin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoVUBench基准，首个专门用于评估大型视觉-语言模型（LVLM）中版权内容遗忘效果的框架，利用程序生成的合法合成数据和系统性的视觉变化来评估遗忘的泛化性，并从版权持有者和部署者双视角衡量遗忘效果与模型效用之间的权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: LVLM在训练后可能记忆并再生版权视觉内容（如角色、标志），机器遗忘技术虽能缓解此风险，但现有评估方法缺乏鲁棒性，且未能捕捉跨模态概念擦除的细微之处，亟需一个标准化、全面的评估基准。

Method: CoVUBench使用程序生成的合法合成数据，并引入系统性视觉变化（包括组合变化和跨领域表现）来模拟真实场景；设计多模态评估协议，从版权持有者角度评估遗忘效果，从部署者角度评估模型通用效用的保持情况，从而量化两者间的权衡。

Result: 通过严格测量遗忘效果与模型效用之间的权衡，CoVUBench为LVLM的负责任遗忘方法提供了标准化评估工具，推动该领域的发展（具体实验结果需参考原文）。

Conclusion: CoVUBench是首个专门针对LVLM版权内容遗忘评估的基准，填补了多模态场景下遗忘评估的空白，为开发负责任且有效的遗忘方法提供了标准化标准化平台。

Original Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs), trained on web-scale data, risk memorizing and regenerating copyrighted visual content such as characters and logos, creating significant challenges. Machine unlearning offers a path to mitigate these risks by removing specific content post-training, but evaluating its effectiveness, especially in the complex multimodal setting of LVLMs, remains an open problem. Current evaluation methods often lack robustness or fail to capture the nuances of cross-modal concept erasure. To address this critical gap, we introduce the CoVUBench benchmark, the first framework specifically designed for evaluating copyright content unlearning in LVLMs. CoVUBench utilizes procedurally generated, legally safe synthetic data coupled with systematic visual variations spanning compositional changes and diverse domain manifestations to ensure realistic and robust evaluation of unlearning generalization. Our comprehensive multimodal evaluation protocol assesses both forgetting efficacy from the copyright holder perspective and the preservation of general model utility from the deployer viewpoint. By rigorously measuring this crucial trade-off, CoVUBench provides a standardized tool to advance the development of responsible and effective unlearning methods for LVLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 大型视觉-语言模型（LVLM）在互联网规模数据上训练时，存在记忆并再生受版权保护的视觉内容（如角色和标志）的风险，这带来了重大挑战。机器遗忘技术提供了一条缓解路径，可在训练后移除特定内容，但如何评估其有效性——尤其是在LVLM复杂的多模态设定下——仍是一个开放问题。当前的评估方法往往缺乏鲁棒性，或未能捕捉跨模态概念擦除的细微差别。为弥补这一关键空白，我们提出了CoVUBench基准，这是首个专门用于评估LVLM中版权内容遗忘效果的框架。CoVUBench利用程序生成的、法律上安全的合成数据，并结合系统性的视觉变化（涵盖组合变化和多样的领域表现），以确保对遗忘泛化性进行真实且鲁棒的评估。我们全面的多模态评估协议从版权持有者视角评估遗忘效果，同时从部署者视角衡量模型通用效用的保持情况。通过严格测量这一关键权衡，CoVUBench为推进LVLM负责任且有效的遗忘方法的发展提供了标准化工具。

</details>


### [32] [AniMatrix: An Anime Video Generation Model that Thinks in Art, Not Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03652)
*Tencent HY Team*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AniMatrix是一个面向动漫视频生成的双通道条件模型，通过重新定义“正确性”、覆盖物理先验、区分艺术与失败的三步策略，实现了符合动漫艺术惯例而非物理真实感的视频生成，在专业动画师评估的五个维度中四项排名第一。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频生成模型内化物理真实感为先验，而动漫有意违反物理规律（如运动模糊、冲击帧、Q版变形），且大量共存的艺术惯例无法被统一建模。物理偏置的模型要么抹平了动漫的艺术性，要么在风格多样性下崩溃。

Method: 提出AniMatrix，核心包括：(1) 双通道条件机制：Production Knowledge System将动漫编码为可控生产变量（风格、运动、相机、VFX）的结构化分类体系，AniCaption从像素推断这些变量；可训练标签编码器保留字段-值结构，冻结T5编码器处理自由叙事文本，通过交叉注意力（细粒度控制）和AdaLN调制（全局强制）双路径注入。(2) 风格-运动-变形课程学习：从近物理运动逐步过渡到完整的动漫表现力。(3) 变形感知偏好优化：通过领域特定奖励模型区分有意艺术性表达与病理学崩溃。

Result: 在专业动画师对五个制作维度的评估中，AniMatrix在四项上排名第一，相较于Seedance-Pro 1.0在提示理解能力上提升+0.70（+22.4%），在艺术运动上提升+0.55（+16.9%）。

Conclusion: 通过重新定义生成目标为艺术正确性而非物理正确性，并设计结构化条件机制与渐进训练策略，AniMatrix有效克服了物理偏置模型在动漫视频生成中的局限性，显著提升了动漫表现力的保真度。

Original Abstract: Video generation models internalize physical realism as their prior. Anime deliberately violates physics: smears, impact frames, chibi shifts; and its thousands of coexisting artistic conventions yield no single "physics of anime" a model can absorb. Physics-biased models therefore flatten the artistry that defines the medium or collapse under its stylistic variance. We present AniMatrix, a video generation model that targets artistic rather than physical correctness through a dual-channel conditioning mechanism and a three-step transition: redefine correctness, override the physics prior, and distinguish art from failure. First, a Production Knowledge System encodes anime as a structured taxonomy of controllable production variables (Style, Motion, Camera, VFX), and AniCaption infers these variables from pixels as directorial directives. A trainable tag encoder preserves the field-value structure of this taxonomy while a frozen T5 encoder handles free-form narrative; dual-path injection (cross-attention for fine-grained control, AdaLN modulation for global enforcement) ensures categorical directives are never diluted by open-ended text. Second, a style-motion-deformation curriculum transitions the model from near-physical motion to full anime expressiveness. Third, deformation-aware preference optimization with a domain-specific reward model separates intentional artistry from pathological collapse. On an anime-specific human evaluation with five production dimensions scored by professional animators, AniMatrix ranks first on four of five, with the largest gains over Seedance-Pro 1.0 on Prompt Understanding (+0.70, +22.4 percent) and Artistic Motion (+0.55, +16.9 percent). We will publicly release the AniMatrix model weights and inference code.

中文摘要翻译: 视频生成模型将物理真实感内化为先验知识。而动漫有意违反物理规律：运动模糊、冲击帧、Q版变形；其数千种共存的艺术惯例使得模型无法吸收单一的“动漫物理法则”。因此，物理偏置的模型要么磨平了定义该媒介的艺术性，要么在其风格多样性下崩溃。我们提出AniMatrix，一种通过双通道条件机制和三步转换（重新定义正确性、覆盖物理先验、区分艺术与失败）来追求艺术正确性而非物理正确性的视频生成模型。首先，Production Knowledge System将动漫编码为可控生产变量（风格、运动、相机、VFX）的结构化分类体系，AniCaption从像素中推断这些变量作为导演指令。可训练的标签编码器保留该分类体系的字段-值结构，而冻结的T5编码器处理自由形式的叙事文本；双路径注入（交叉注意力用于细粒度控制，AdaLN调制用于全局强制）确保分类指令不会被开放式文本稀释。其次，风格-运动-变形课程学习将模型从近物理运动过渡到完整的动漫表现力。第三，变形感知偏好优化结合领域特定奖励模型，将有意的艺术性表达与病理性崩溃区分开。在由专业动画师对五个制作维度进行的动漫特定人工评估中，AniMatrix在四项上排名第一，相较于Seedance-Pro 1.0的最大增益体现在提示理解能力（+0.70，+22.4%）和艺术运动（+0.55，+16.9%）。我们将公开发布AniMatrix模型权重和推理代码。

</details>


### [33] [Unified Multimodal Visual Tracking with Dual Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03716)
*Lingyi Hong,Jinglun Li,Xinyu Zhou,Kaixun Jiang,Pinxue Guo,Zhaoyu Chen,Runze Li,Xingdong Sheng,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OneTrackerV2 是一个统一的多模态视觉目标跟踪框架，通过 Meta Merger 嵌入多模态信息、Dual Mixture-of-Experts 解耦时空关系与多模态知识，以单一架构和端到端训练在五个跟踪任务和12个基准上达到 SOTA，并具备高推理效率和模态缺失鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法针对不同输入模态（如 RGB、RGB+X）训练独立模型或依赖预训练模型进行适配，导致效率低、可扩展性差、可用性受限。本文旨在构建一个统一的、支持任意模态端到端训练的多模态跟踪框架。

Method: 提出 OneTrackerV2 统一框架，包含：(1) Meta Merger，将多模态信息嵌入统一空间，实现灵活融合与鲁棒性；(2) Dual Mixture-of-Experts (DMoE)，其中 T-MoE 建模时空关系用于跟踪，M-MoE 嵌入多模态知识，解耦跨模态依赖并减少特征冲突。采用共享架构、统一参数和单一端到端训练。

Result: 在五种 RGB 和 RGB+X 跟踪任务、12个基准上达到 SOTA 性能，推理效率高；模型压缩后仍保持强性能；在模态缺失场景下表现出显著的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: OneTrackerV2 以统一架构和端到端训练有效解决了多模态跟踪中的效率与可扩展性问题，在多个任务和基准上超越现有方法，同时兼具模型压缩后的稳定性与模态缺失下的鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Multimodal visual object tracking can be divided into to several kinds of tasks (e.g. RGB and RGB+X tracking), based on the input modality. Existing methods often train separate models for each modality or rely on pretrained models to adapt to new modalities, which limits efficiency, scalability, and usability. Thus, we introduce OneTrackerV2, a unified multi-modal tracking framework that enables end-to-end training for any modality. We propose Meta Merger to embed multi-modal information into a unified space, allowing flexible modality fusion and robustness. We further introduce Dual Mixture-of-Experts (DMoE): T-MoE models spatio-temporal relations for tracking, while M-MoE embeds multi-modal knowledge, disentangling cross-modal dependencies and reducing feature conflicts. With a shared architecture, unified parameters, and a single end-to-end training, OneTrackerV2 achieves state-of-the-art performance across five RGB and RGB+X tracking tasks and 12 benchmarks, while maintaining high inference efficiency. Notably, even after model compression, OneTrackerV2 retains strong performance. Moreover, OneTrackerV2 demonstrates remarkable robustness under modality-missing scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态视觉目标跟踪可根据输入模态分为多种任务（例如 RGB 和 RGB+X 跟踪）。现有方法常针对每种模态单独训练模型，或依赖预训练模型适配新模态，这限制了效率、可扩展性和可用性。为此，我们提出 OneTrackerV2，一个统一的多模态跟踪框架，支持任意模态的端到端训练。我们提出 Meta Merger，将多模态信息嵌入统一空间，实现灵活的模态融合与鲁棒性。我们进一步引入双混合专家（DMoE）：T-MoE 建模时空关系用于跟踪，M-MoE 嵌入多模态知识，解耦跨模态依赖并减少特征冲突。凭借共享架构、统一参数和单一端到端训练，OneTrackerV2 在五种 RGB 和 RGB+X 跟踪任务及12个基准上取得了最先进的性能，同时保持高推理效率。值得注意的是，即使在模型压缩后，OneTrackerV2 仍保持强劲性能。此外，OneTrackerV2 在模态缺失场景下展现出显著的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [34] [FluxFlow: Conservative Flow-Matching for Astronomical Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03749)
*Shuhong Liu,Xining Ge,Ziteng Cui,Liuzhuozheng Li,Gengjia Chang,Jun Liu,Ziying Gu,Dong Li,Xuangeng Chu,Lin Gu,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FluxFlow框架，通过保守的像素空间流匹配、观测不确定性加权和无训练测试时维纳正则化校正，实现地从空超分辨率；同时构建了包含19,500对真实地-空图像对的DESI-HST基准数据集，在光度和科学精度上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法依赖合成训练对，无法捕捉真实大气统计特性，容易产生过度平滑或幻觉伪影（即恢复出天空中不存在的源）。

Method: 提出FluxFlow框架，包含三个核心：1) 保守的像素空间流匹配模型，训练时融入观测不确定性和源区域重要性权重；2) 无训练测试时维纳正则化校正，抑制幻觉源同时保留恢复细节。

Result: 构建DESI-HST真实世界基准数据集（19,500对真实配准的地-空图像对，含真实大气PSF变化）；实验表明FluxFlow在光度和科学精度上均持续优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: FluxFlow通过结合不确定性感知训练和测试时正则化校正，能够有效解决真实大气条件下的超分辨率问题，避免过度平滑和幻觉，在真实数据上取得更优性能。

Original Abstract: Ground-to-space astronomical super-resolution requires recovering space-quality images from ground-based observations that are simultaneously limited by pixel sampling resolution and atmospheric seeing, which imposes a stochastic, spatially varying PSF that cannot be resolved through upsampling alone. Existing methods rely on synthetic training pairs that fail to capture real atmospheric statistics and are prone to either over-smoothed reconstructions or hallucination sources with no physical counterpart in the observed sky. We propose FluxFlow, a conservative pixel-space flow-matching framework that incorporates observation uncertainty and source-region importance weights during training, and a training-free Wiener-regularized test-time correction to suppress hallucination sources while preserving recovered detail. We further construct the DESI--HST Dataset, the large-scale real-world benchmark comprising 19,500 real co-registered ground-to-space image pairs with real atmospheric PSF variation. Experiments demonstrate that FluxFlow consistently outperforms existing baseline methods in both photometric and scientific accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 地到空天文超分辨率需要从地面观测中恢复空间质量的图像，这些观测同时受到像素采样分辨率和大气视宁度的限制，后者施加了一个随机的、空间变化的点扩散函数（PSF），仅通过上采样无法解析。现有方法依赖无法捕捉真实大气统计特性的合成训练对，并且容易产生过度平滑的重建结果或在观测天空中不存在物理对应源的幻觉源。我们提出了FluxFlow，一个保守的像素空间流匹配框架，在训练中融入观测不确定性和源区域重要性权重，并引入一种无训练、基于维纳正则化的测试时校正方法，在抑制幻觉源的同时保留恢复的细节。此外，我们构建了DESI-HST数据集，这是包含19,500对真实配准的地-空图像对的大规模真实世界基准数据集，具有真实的大气PSF变化。实验表明，FluxFlow在光度和科学精度上均持续优于现有基线方法。

</details>


### [35] [Before Forgetting, Learn to Remember: Revisiting Foundational Learning Failures in LVLM Unlearning Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03759)
*JuneHyoung Kwon,MiHyeon Kim,Eunju Lee,JungMin Yun,Byeonggeuk Lim,YoungBin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出当前LVLM遗忘基准存在“阶段1失败”（模型未能有效记忆目标信息），诊断出欠记忆和多跳诅咒两大根本原因，提出ReMem基准（包含原则性数据扩展、推理感知问答对、多样化视觉上下文）和Exposure指标（从内部概率分布量化信息擦除深度），为LVLM的学习和遗忘行为提供可靠评估框架。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LVLM遗忘基准使用虚构身份来评估隐私遗忘效果，但忽略了关键问题：模型最初就未能有效记忆目标信息（阶段1失败），这使得后续的遗忘评估不可靠。因此需要更可靠的基准来同时诊断学习和遗忘行为。

Method: 提出ReMem基准：1）原则性数据扩展确保基础学习；2）构建推理感知的QA对；3）引入多样化视觉上下文。同时提出Exposure指标，从模型内部概率分布量化信息擦除深度。

Result: 大量实验表明ReMem为诊断LVLM的学习和遗忘行为提供了严谨可信的框架。

Conclusion: ReMem通过对学习和遗忘的双重诊断，解决了现有基准因欠记忆导致的评估不可靠问题，为LVLM隐私保护研究提供了更严格的评估标准。

Original Abstract: While Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) offer powerful capabilities, they pose privacy risks by unintentionally memorizing sensitive personal information. Current unlearning benchmarks attempt to mitigate this using fictitious identities but overlook a critical stage 1 failure: models fail to effectively memorize target information initially, rendering subsequent unlearning evaluations unreliable. Diagnosing under-memorization and the multi-hop curse as root causes, we introduce ReMem, a Reliable Multi-hop and Multi-image Memorization Benchmark. ReMem ensures robust foundational learning through principled data scaling, reasoning-aware QA pairs, and diverse visual contexts. Additionally, we propose a novel Exposure metric to quantify the depth of information erasure from the model's internal probability distribution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReMem provides a rigorous and trustworthy framework for diagnosing both learning and unlearning behaviors in LVLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 大型视觉-语言模型（LVLM）虽然提供了强大的能力，但会无意中记忆敏感的个人信息，从而带来隐私风险。当前的遗忘基准尝试使用虚构身份来缓解这一问题，却忽略了一个关键的阶段1失败：模型最初未能有效记忆目标信息，导致后续的遗忘评估不可靠。我们诊断出欠记忆和多跳诅咒是根本原因，并提出了ReMem——一个可靠的多跳和多图像记忆基准。ReMem通过原则性数据扩展、推理感知的问答对以及多样化的视觉上下文来确保稳健的基础学习。此外，我们提出了一种新颖的Exposure指标，从模型内部概率分布量化信息擦除的深度。大量实验表明，ReMem为诊断LVLM中的学习和遗忘行为提供了严谨可信的框架。

</details>


### [36] [GeoTopoDiff: Learning Geometry--Topology Graph Priors through Boundary-Constrained Mixed Diffusion for Sparse-Slice 3D Porous Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03764)
*Yue Shi,Peng Wang,Mingzhe Yu,Yunlong Zhao,Li Liu,Gareth D Hatton,Yan Lyu,Liangxiu Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoTopoDiff，一种基于图扩散的框架，将扩散先验从体素空间迁移到混合图状态空间，用于从稀疏CT切片重建3D多孔微结构，同时建模连续孔隙几何和离散孔喉拓扑。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于体素的扩散模型需要完整的CT扫描才能提供拓扑保真的先验，导致在实际工业应用中通量、拓扑保真度和视野之间存在固有权衡；且同时建模连续孔隙形态和离散孔喉拓扑极具挑战性。

Method: 提出GeoTopoDiff框架，将扩散先验学习从体素空间迁移到混合图状态空间，该空间同时包含连续孔隙几何和离散孔喉拓扑；引入拓扑感知的部分图先验，利用稀疏CT切片约束逆向去噪过程。

Result: 在各向异性PTFE和Fontainebleau砂岩上的实验表明，GeoTopoDiff平均减少形态相关误差19.8%，减少拓扑敏感输运误差36.5%。

Conclusion: 混合图状态空间能够促进扩散去噪过程，在稀疏观测条件下降低后验不确定性；所有模型和代码已开源以便进扩散模型在3D多孔微结构模拟领域的探索。

Original Abstract: Diffusion-based voxel prior modelling is challenging for the reconstruction of large-scale 3D porous microstructures. Due to the demanding requirements for simultaneously modelling both the continuous pore morphology and the discrete pore-throat topology, the diffusion models require fully observed CT scans to provide topology-faithful priors, which results in an inherent trade-off among throughput, topological fidelity, and field of view in practical industrial applications. We propose GeoTopoDiff, a graph diffusion-based framework for reconstructing 3D porous microstructures from sparse CT slices. GeoTopoDiff transfers the learning of diffusion priors from a voxel-based space to a mixed graph state space, which simultaneously encompasses continuous pore geometry and discrete pore-throat topology. A topology-aware partial graph prior from sparsely observed CT slices is introduced to constrain the reverse denoising process. Experiments on anisotropic PTFE and Fontainebleau sandstone show that GeoTopoDiff reduces morphology-related errors by 19.8% and topology-sensitive transport errors by 36.5% on average. Our findings suggest that the mixed graph state space promotes the diffusion denoising process to reduce posterior uncertainty under a sparse observations. All models and code have been made publicly available to facilitate the exploration of diffusion models in the field of 3D porous microstructures simulation.

中文摘要翻译: 基于体素的扩散先验建模对于大规模3D多孔微结构的重建具有挑战性。由于同时建模连续孔隙形态和离散孔喉拓扑的严苛要求，扩散模型需要完全观测的CT扫描来提供拓扑保真的先验，这导致在实际工业应用中通量、拓扑保真度和视野之间存在固有权衡。我们提出GeoTopoDiff，一种基于图扩散的框架，用于从稀疏CT切片重建3D多孔微结构。GeoTopoDiff将扩散先验的学习从基于体素的空间迁移到混合图状态空间，该空间同时包含连续孔隙几何和离散孔喉拓扑。引入了来自稀疏观测CT切片的拓扑感知部分图先验来约束逆向去噪过程。在各向异性PTFE和Fontainebleau砂岩上的实验表明，GeoTopoDiff平均减少形态相关误差19.8%，减少拓扑敏感输运误差36.5%。我们的发现表明，混合图状态空间能够促进扩散去噪过程，在稀疏观测条件下降低后验不确定性。所有模型和代码已公开，以促进扩散模型在3D多孔微结构模拟领域中的探索。

</details>


### [37] [ReLeaf: Benchmarking Leaf Segmentation across Domains and Species](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03784)
*Robert Martinko,Daniel Steininger,Julia Simon,Andreas Trondl,Matthias Blaickner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了现有叶片分割数据集和实例分割架构，提出了一种YOLO26模型配置在精度农业任务中取得最佳权衡，并发布了涵盖23个植物物种的新基准数据集，揭示了跨域泛化中的显著性能下降问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 全球粮食需求增长和气候压力需要更可持续、精准的农业实践，而细粒度的叶片级视觉分析对于评估作物健康状况、生长动态、产量潜力和局部胁迫症状至关重要，但叶片级分割尚未得到充分研究，主要受限于缺乏专用数据集和系统的架构评估。

Method: 调研并筛选出四个公开可用的叶片分割数据集，比较了单阶段、两阶段和基于Transformer的检测器，确定YOLO26模型配置提供最佳精度-速度权衡；通过半自动标注方式创建了涵盖23个植物物种的新基准数据集（基于CropAndWeed图像），并在多个数据集上进行跨域泛化实验。

Result: 在四个现有数据集的测试集上，最优模型平均mAP50-95达83.9%；在新基准数据集上为40.2%；跨域泛化实验显示在不同植物物种和记录设置下性能显著下降，特别是仅在实验室数据上训练的模型。

Conclusion: 叶片级分割在精准农业中具有重要价值但面临泛化挑战，多样化的叶片分割数据集对于构建鲁棒的精准农业模型至关重要，YOLO26在当前任务中提供了最佳的性能-效率权衡。

Original Abstract: Rising global food demand and growing climate pressure increase the need for sustainable, precise agricultural practices. Automated, individualized plant treatment relies on fine-grained visual analysis, yet leaf-level segmentation remains underexplored despite its value for assessing crop health, growth dynamics, yield potential and localized stress symptoms. Progress is limited by a lack of dedicated datasets, especially regarding species coverage, and by the absence of systematic evaluations of modern instance-segmentation architectures for this task. We address these gaps by surveying current data and identifying four suitable, publicly available leaf-segmentation datasets. Using them, we compare one-stage, two-stage and Transformer-based detectors and identify a YOLO26 model configuration to provide the best trade-off for real-world precision-agriculture tasks. Extensive cross-domain generalization experiments reveal substantial performance drops across plant species and recording setups, especially for models trained solely on laboratory data. To strengthen data availability, we introduce a new benchmark dataset with leaf-level masks for 23 plant species, created via semi-automatic annotation of selected CropAndWeed images. A model trained on all four existing datasets achieves a mean mAP50-95 of 83.9% across their corresponding test sets and 40.2% on our new benchmark, demonstrating improved generalization and highlighting the need for diverse leaf-segmentation datasets in robust precision agriculture.

中文摘要翻译: 全球粮食需求不断增长以及日益增加的气候压力，催生了对可持续、精准农业实践的更高需求。自动化、个体化的植物处理依赖于细粒度的视觉分析，然而叶片级分割尽管对评估作物健康状况、生长动态、产量潜力及局部胁迫症状具有重要价值，却仍未得到充分研究。进展受限于专用数据集的缺乏，特别是在物种覆盖范围方面，以及缺乏针对此类任务中现代实例分割架构的系统性评估。为弥补这些不足，我们调研了现有数据，筛选出四个合适的公开叶片分割数据集。基于这些数据集，我们比较了单阶段、两阶段和基于Transformer的检测器，确定了一种YOLO26模型配置，可为实际精准农业任务提供最佳的权衡。广泛的跨域泛化实验表明，模型在不同植物物种和记录设置下的性能显著下降，尤其是仅在实验室数据上训练的模型。为增强数据可用性，我们引入了一个新的基准数据集，包含23种植物物种的叶片级掩膜，该数据集通过对选定的CropAndWeed图像进行半自动标注创建。在全部四个现有数据集上训练的模型，在其对应测试集上平均mAP50-95达到83.9%，在新基准数据集上达到40.2%，展现了改进的泛化能力，同时凸显了多样化叶片分割数据集在构建鲁棒精准农业系统中的重要性。

</details>


### [38] [A Robust Unsupervised Domain Adaptation Framework for Medical Image Classification Using RKHS-MMD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03787)
*Sapna Sachan,Rakesh Kumar Sanodiya,Amulya Kumar Mahto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合迁移学习与RKHS最大均值差异的无监督域适应框架，用于解决医学影像标注瓶颈和域偏移问题，在胸部X光数据集上验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 医学图像标注需要领域专业知识，且不同医疗中心和成像设备带来的域偏移与模态差异限制了模型的泛化能力，亟需一种无需人工标注的域适应方法。

Method: 提出无监督域适应框架：结合迁移学习与基于再生核希尔伯特空间的最大均值差异（RKHS-MMD）损失，通过联合优化分类损失和RKHS-MMD损失对齐源域与目标域分布。

Result: 在两个不同医疗中心的胸部X光数据集上，该方法相比未做域适应的模型有显著提升；对比实验表明RKHS-MMD在缩小模态差距上优于标准MMD。

Conclusion: 该框架有效增强了模型对未标注医学数据集的泛化能力，降低了对人工标注的依赖，在医学图像分类中表现出色，在AI驱动医学诊断中具有潜力。

Original Abstract: Labeling medical images is a major bottleneck in the field of medical imaging, as it requires domain-specific expertise, and it gets further complicated due to variability across different medical centers and different imaging devices. Such heterogeneity introduces domain shifts and modality discrepancies, which limits the generalization of trained models. To address this important challenge, we propose an unsupervised domain adaptation framework that combines transfer learning with a Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space based Maximum Mean Discrepancy loss for the alignment of source and target domains. By jointly optimizing classification and RKHS-MMD losses, the methodology enhances generalization to unannotated medical datasets while diminishing reliance on manual annotation. Experimental evaluations presented on two chest X-ray datasets, which are obtained from different medical centers, show outstanding improvements over models trained without adaptation. Furthermore, we perform a comparative study to see that RKHS-MMD performs better than the standard Maximum Mean Discrepancy in reducing modality gap, emphasizing its effectiveness for medical image classification and also its strong capability in advanced AI-driven medical diagnostics.

中文摘要翻译: 标注医学图像是医学成像领域的主要瓶颈，因为它需要领域专业知识，并且由于不同医疗中心和不同成像设备的差异而变得更加复杂。这种异质性引入了域偏移和模态差异，限制了训练模型的泛化能力。为应对这一重要挑战，我们提出了一种无监督域适应框架，该框架结合了迁移学习与基于再生核希尔伯特空间的最大均值差异损失，用于对齐源域和目标域。通过联合优化分类损失和RKHS-MMD损失，该方法增强了对未标注医学数据集的泛化能力，同时减少了对人工标注的依赖。在两个来自不同医疗中心的胸部X光数据集上进行的实验评估表明，该方法相比未经过域适应训练的模型有显著改进。此外，我们进行的比较研究表明，RKHS-MMD在缩小模态差距方面优于标准最大均值差异，突显了其在医学图像分类中的有效性及其在先进AI驱动医学诊断中的强大能力。

</details>


### [39] [Enhancing Visual Question Answering with Multimodal LLMs via Chain-of-Question Guided Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03790)
*Quanxing Xu,Ling Zhou,Xian Zhong,Xiaohua Huang,Rubing Huang,Chia-Wen Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合链式思维推理与视觉问题分解的逻辑提示策略（CoVQD），并在此基础上构建CgRAG框架，以增强多模态大语言模型在检索式视觉问答中的结构化推理与知识获取能力，在多个基准上验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLMs在开放域VQA中依赖外部知识，但检索到的知识往往不够准确和相关，缺乏结构化推理引导，导致模型在复杂跨领域场景中泛化能力和可靠性不足。

Method: 提出CoVQD（链式思维推理+视觉问题分解）逻辑提示策略，融合CoT推理与VQD以引导检索获取更准确的知识；基于此构建CgRAG框架，使MLLMs在结构化视觉-文本推理指导下访问更全面连贯的外部知识。

Result: 在E-VQA、InfoSeek和OKVQA三个基准上的大量实验证明了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: CoVQD引导的检索增强生成框架能够有效提升MLLMs在复杂跨领域VQA场景中的泛化能力和可靠性。

Original Abstract: With advances in multimodal research and deep learning, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for a wide range of multimodal tasks. As a core problem in vision-language research, Visual Question Answering (VQA) has increasingly employed MLLMs to improve performance, particularly in open-domain settings where external knowledge is essential. In this work, we aim to further enhance retrieval-based VQA by more effectively integrating MLLMs with structured reasoning and knowledge acquisition. We introduce a logical prompting strategy that fuses Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning with Visual Question Decomposition (VQD), termed CoVQD, to guide retrieval toward more accurate and relevant knowledge for MLLM inference. Building on this idea, we propose a new framework, CoVQD-guided RAG (CgRAG), which enables MLLMs to access more comprehensive and coherent external knowledge while benefiting from structured visual-text reasoning guidance, thereby improving generalization and reliability in complex cross-domain VQA scenarios. Extensive experiments on E-VQA, InfoSeek, and OKVQA benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

中文摘要翻译: 随着多模态研究和深度学习的进步，多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）已成为广泛多模态任务中一种强大的范式。作为视觉-语言研究的核心问题，视觉问答（VQA）越来越多地采用MLLMs来提升性能，特别是在需要外部知识的开放域场景中。本文旨在通过更有效地将MLLMs与结构化推理和知识获取相结合，进一步增强基于检索的VQA。我们提出了一种逻辑提示策略，将链式思维（CoT）推理与视觉问题分解（VQD）相融合，称之为CoVQD，以引导检索为MLLM推理获取更准确、更相关的知识。基于这一思想，我们提出了一种新框架——CoVQD引导的RAG（CgRAG），该框架使MLLMs能够在结构化视觉-文本推理指导下访问更全面、更连贯的外部知识，从而提升在复杂跨领域VQA场景中的泛化能力和可靠性。在E-VQA、InfoSeek和OKVQA基准上的大量实验证明了所提方法的有效性。

</details>


### [40] [Multimodal Learning on Low-Quality Data with Conformal Predictive Self-Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03820)
*Xun Jiang,Yufan Gu,Disen Hu,Yuqing Hou,Yazhou Yao,Fumin Shen,Heng Tao Shen,Xing Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了统一框架CPSC，利用共形预测实现多模态学习中的表征自校准和梯度自校准，同时应对模态不平衡和噪声污染问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态学习中，模态不平衡和噪声污染是两大挑战，现有方法通常孤立研究两者，但它们在预测不确定性上存在共同根源，需要统一框架同时解决。

Method: 提出CPSC框架，包括：(1) 表征自校准模块，解构单模态特征并利用共形预测器选择最鲁棒的组件进行融合；(2) 梯度自校准模块，基于实例级可靠性分数在反向传播中重新校准梯度流；(3) 共形预测器的自更新策略，确保整个系统在训练过程中协同演化。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，CPSC在模态不平衡和噪声两种设置下均一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过统一框架同时解决模态不平衡和噪声污染问题，验证了共形预测在多模态学习中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Multimodal learning often grapples with the challenge of low-quality data, which predominantly manifests as two facets: modality imbalance and noisy corruption. While these issues are often studied in isolation, we argue that they share a common root in the predictive uncertainty towards the reliability of individual modalities and instances during learning. In this paper, we propose a unified framework, termed Conformal Predictive Self-Calibration (CPSC), which leverages conformal prediction to equip the model with the ability to perform self-guided calibration on-the-fly. The core of our proposed CPSC lies in a novel self-calibrating training loop that seamlessly integrates two key modules: (1) Representation Self-Calibration, which decomposes unimodal features into components, and selectively fuses the most robust ones identified by a conformal predictor to enhance feature resilience. (2) Gradient Self-Calibration, which recalibrates the gradient flow during backpropagation based on instance-wise reliability scores, steering the optimization towards more trustworthy directions. Furthermore, we also devise a self-update strategy for the conformal predictor to ensure the entire system co-evolves consistently throughout the training process. Extensive experiments on six benchmark datasets under both imbalanced and noisy settings demonstrate that our CPSC framework consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/XunCHN/CPSC.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态学习常常面临低质量数据的挑战，主要表现为两个层面：模态不平衡和噪声污染。尽管这些问题通常被孤立研究，但我们认为它们在预测个体模态和实例在学习过程中的可靠性方面具有共同的不确定性根源。在本文中，我们提出了一个统一框架，称为共形预测自校准（CPSC），该框架利用共形预测使模型具备在训练过程中进行自主引导校准的能力。CPSC的核心在于一种新颖的自校准训练循环，该循环无缝集成了两个关键模块：(1) 表征自校准，将单模态特征分解为组件，并由共形预测器识别出最鲁棒的组件进行选择性融合，以增强特征的韧性；(2) 梯度自校准，基于实例级可靠性分数在反向传播过程中重新校准梯度流，将优化导向更可信的方向。此外，我们还设计了共形预测器的自更新策略，以确保整个系统在整个训练过程中协同演化。在六个基准数据集上，分别在不平衡和噪声设置下进行的大量实验表明，我们的CPSC框架一致优于现有最先进方法。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/XunCHN/CPSC 获取。

</details>


### [41] [Identity-Consistent Multi-Pose Generation of Contactless Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03830)
*Zhiyu Pan,Xiongjun Guan,Jianjiang Feng,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IMPOSE框架，通过三阶段流程（潜扩散生成卷指印身份、Sauvola二值化引导的跨模态转换、3D物理模拟多姿态投影）合成身份一致的多姿态非接触指纹样本，在UWA和PolyU数据集上将跨模态匹配EER分别降至8.74%和2.26%，达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 非接触指纹识别因缺乏物理约束，自由手指姿态导致严重的非线性几何畸变，造成与接触式指纹间的巨大跨模态域差距。现有显式几何校正或图像增强方法在极端姿态下表现脆弱，亟需一种能生成身份一致、多姿态非接触指纹样本来增强识别模型的方法。

Method: IMPOSE包含三个阶段：(1) 基于潜扩散与离散码本表示的卷指印身份生成；(2) 以Sauvola局部自适应二值化为身份锚点，将卷指印跨模态转换为非接触模态；(3) 通过3D手指模型纹理映射与投影进行基于物理的多姿态模拟。

Result: 在UWA数据集上EER降至8.74%，PolyU CL2CB数据集上EER降至2.26%，均达到SOTA；合成数据在DeepPrint、AFRNet等主流表示上均有一致性提升，合成+真实数据的混合策略取得最佳结果。

Conclusion: 所提IMPOSE框架能生成脊线拓扑层面身份一致、空间对齐标准指纹坐标的多姿态非接触指纹样本，有效弥合跨模态域差距，显著提升非接触与接触式指纹的跨模态匹配性能。

Original Abstract: Contactless fingerprint recognition has gained increasing attention due to its advantages in hygiene and acquisition flexibility. However, the absence of physical contact constraints introduces severe nonlinear geometric distortions caused by free finger poses in 3D space, resulting in a substantial cross-modal domain gap between contactless and conventional contact-based fingerprints. Existing solutions largely rely on explicit geometric correction or image enhancement, which are fragile under extreme pose variations. In this paper, we propose Identity-Consistent Multi-Pose Generation of Contactless Fingerprints (IMPOSE), a physics-inspired framework that synthesizes identity-preserving, multi-pose contactless fingerprint samples to empower recognition models. IMPOSE consists of three stages: (1) rolled fingerprint identity generation via latent diffusion with discrete codebook representations, (2) cross-modal translation from rolled to contactless modality guided by Sauvola-based local adaptive binarization as an identity anchor, and (3) physics-based multi-pose simulation through 3D finger model texture mapping and projection. The generated samples maintain strict identity consistency at the ridge topology level and spatial alignment with standard fingerprint coordinate space. Extensive experiments on the UWA and PolyU CL2CB databases demonstrate that fine-tuning fixed-length dense descriptors (FDD) with IMPOSE-synthesized data achieves state-of-the-art cross-modal matching, reducing EER to 8.74% on UWA and 2.26% on PolyU CL2CB. Synthetic data also yields consistent gains across mainstream representations including DeepPrint and AFRNet, and the hybrid strategy combining synthetic and real data achieves the best overall results. The code and generated samples are available at https://github.com/Yu-Yy/IMPOSE.

中文摘要翻译: 非接触指纹识别因其卫生优势和采集灵活性而受到越来越多的关注。然而，缺乏物理接触约束会导致由三维空间中自由手指姿态引起的严重非线性几何畸变，从而在非接触式与传统接触式指纹之间产生巨大的跨模态域差距。现有解决方案大多依赖显式几何校正或图像增强，这些方法在极端姿态变化下表现脆弱。本文提出非接触指纹的身份一致多姿态生成方法（IMPOSE），这是一个受物理启发的框架，能够合成保持身份的多姿态非接触指纹样本以增强识别模型。IMPOSE包含三个阶段：(1) 通过基于离散码本表示的潜扩散生成卷指印身份；(2) 以Sauvola局部自适应二值化作为身份锚点，指导从卷指印到非接触模态的跨模态转换；(3) 通过3D手指模型纹理映射与投影进行基于物理的多姿态模拟。生成的样本在脊线拓扑层面保持严格的身份一致性，并与标准指纹坐标空间对齐。在UWA和PolyU CL2CB数据库上的大量实验表明，使用IMPOSE合成数据微调固定长度密集描述子（FDD）实现了最先进的跨模态匹配性能，在UWA上将等错误率降至8.74%，在PolyU CL2CB上降至2.26%。合成数据在DeepPrint和AFRNet等主流表示上也能带来一致的性能提升，而合成数据与真实数据相结合的混合策略取得了最佳总体结果。代码和生成的样本可在https://github.com/Yu-Yy/IMPOSE获取。

</details>


### [42] [Conditions for well-posed color recovery in scattering media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03837)
*Grigory Solomatov,Derya Akkaynak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了散射介质中场景颜色恢复的适定性问题，证明了在理想高光谱相机下，通过利用图像中自然存在的跨像素关系（恢复模式），可以唯一确定颜色解，从而为散射环境下的定量成像提供了理论基础。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 散射介质中的颜色恢复本质上是不适定的（ill-posed），因为同一观测结果可能对应多个解，且无法控制预测误差。现有方法缺乏对候选解空间的理解，因此需要找到使问题适定的充分条件。

Method: 通过分析不适定性的两个根源——(i) 光谱信号投影到像素强度时的信息损失，以及(ii) 未知的介质参数——证明仅靠传感器改进无法解决介质引起的畸变。进而识别出图像中自然存在的跨像素关系（恢复模式），并在理想高光谱相机条件下证明这些模式能将解限制为唯一候选解。

Result: 证明了在理想高光谱相机下，利用图像中固有的跨像素关系（恢复模式）可以将散射介质中的颜色恢复问题从不适定变为适定，即存在唯一解。

Conclusion: 该发现为基于第一性原理的新型视觉算法开辟了道路，使得散射环境下的图像定量分析成为可能。

Original Abstract: Recovering scene color from images captured in scattering media is a fundamental inverse problem in optical imaging. Yet the problem is intrinsically ill-posed as multiple solutions can explain the same observation, and prediction error cannot be controlled without understanding the space of candidate solutions. Here, we present sufficient conditions under which color recovery in a scattering medium becomes well-posed. Observing that ill-posedness stems from (i) projection of spectral signals onto pixel intensities, and (ii) unknown medium parameters, we demonstrate that sensor improvements alone cannot resolve medium-induced distortions without additional constraints. We identify recovery patterns, cross-pixel relationships that naturally occur in images, and prove, for an ideal hyperspectral camera, that they restrict the solution to a unique candidate. This opens the door to a new class of vision algorithms grounded in first principles, enabling quantitative analysis of images in scattering environments.

中文摘要翻译: 从散射介质中拍摄的图像恢复场景颜色是光学成像中的一个基本逆问题。然而，该问题本质上是不适定的，因为同一观测结果可能对应多个解，并且在没有理解候选解空间的情况下无法控制预测误差。在本文中，我们提出了散射介质中颜色恢复问题变得适定的充分条件。通过观察到不适定性源于 (i) 光谱信号投影到像素强度上的信息损失，以及 (ii) 未知的介质参数，我们证明仅靠传感器改进无法在没有额外约束的条件下解决介质引起的畸变。我们识别出恢复模式——图像中自然出现的跨像素关系——并证明在理想高光谱相机下，这些模式将解限制为唯一候选解。这为基于第一性原理的新型视觉算法开辟了道路，使得散射环境下的定量图像分析成为可能。

</details>


### [43] [Parameter-Efficient Multi-View Proficiency Estimation: From Discriminative Classification to Generative Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03848)
*Edoardo Bianchi,Antonio Liotta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了近期三项针对Ego-Exo4D数据集的多视角技能水平估计工作：SkillFormer（参数高效判别架构与选择性多视图融合）、PATS（基于运动基元的密集时序采样）和ProfVLM（将技能估计转化为条件语言生成）。三者以更少参数和训练轮次实现SOTA精度，并推动从封闭分类向可解释反馈生成的转变。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统动作识别仅关注分类，但技能水平评估（如动作熟练度）在教练、康复和人才识别中至关重要，其挑战在于熟练度编码于时序、平衡、身体力学等细微差异中，且常分布于多视角和短时事件中。

Method: SkillFormer采用参数高效的判别架构实现选择性多视图融合；PATS通过保留基础运动片段的局部密集样本来改进时序采样；ProfVLM将技能水平估计重构为条件语言生成，通过门控跨视角投影器和小型语言骨干同时输出技能标签和专家级反馈。

Result: 在Ego-Exo4D上达到SOTA精度，可训练参数减少至1/20，训练轮次减少至1/3，并从封闭集分类转向可解释的反馈生成。

Conclusion: 这些成果标志着向高效、多视角系统的转变，该系统结合了选择性融合、技能感知采样和可操作的生成式反馈。

Original Abstract: Estimating how well a person performs an action, rather than which action is performed, is central to coaching, rehabilitation, and talent identification. This task is challenging because proficiency is encoded in subtle differences in timing, balance, body mechanics, and execution, often distributed across multiple views and short temporal events. We discuss three recent contributions to multi-view proficiency estimation on Ego-Exo4D. SkillFormer introduces a parameter-efficient discriminative architecture for selective multi-view fusion; PATS improves temporal sampling by preserving locally dense excerpts of fundamental movements; and ProfVLM reformulates proficiency estimation as conditional language generation, producing both a proficiency label and expert-style feedback through a gated cross-view projector and a compact language backbone. Together, these methods achieve state-of-the-art accuracy on Ego-Exo4D with up to 20x fewer trainable parameters and up to 3x fewer training epochs than video-transformer baselines, while moving from closed-set classification toward interpretable feedback generation. These results highlight a shift toward efficient, multi-view systems that combine selective fusion, proficiency-aware sampling, and actionable generative feedback.

中文摘要翻译: 评估一个人执行动作的熟练程度（而非识别执行了什么动作）在教练指导、康复训练和人才识别中至关重要。这一任务具有挑战性，因为熟练度编码于时序、平衡、身体力学和执行的细微差异中，且通常分布在多个视角和短时事件中。我们讨论了近期针对Ego-Exo4D数据集的三个多视角熟练度估计贡献：SkillFormer引入了一种参数高效的判别架构用于选择性多视图融合；PATS通过保留基础运动片段的局部密集样本来改进时序采样；ProfVLM将熟练度估计重构为条件语言生成，通过门控跨视角投影器和小型语言骨干同时输出熟练度标签和专家级反馈。综合来看，这些方法在Ego-Exo4D上达到了最先进的精度，可训练参数比视频Transformer基线减少多达20倍，训练轮次减少多达3倍，同时从封闭集分类转向可解释的反馈生成。这些结果突显了向高效多视图系统的转变，该系统结合了选择性融合、熟练度感知采样和可操作的生成式反馈。

</details>


### [44] [Stream-R1: Reliability-Perplexity Aware Reward Distillation for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03849)
*Bin Wu,Mengqi Huang,Shaojin Wu,Weinan Jia,Yuxin Wang,Zhendong Mao,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Stream-R1，通过可靠性-困惑度感知的奖励蒸馏框架，在回滚和时空元素两个层面自适应重加权蒸馏目标，无需架构修改或额外推理成本即可提升视频扩散模型的蒸馏质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于蒸馏的加速方法对教师模型的输出不加区分地学习，将所有回滚、帧和像素视为同等可靠的监督信号，忽略了DMD监督中两个互补的方差维度：不同学生回滚之间的监督可靠性差异（Inter-Reliability），以及空间区域和时间帧对质量改进贡献的不均衡性（Intra-Perplexity）。这限制了蒸馏质量的上限。

Method: 提出Stream-R1框架，通过共享的奖励引导机制自适应地重加权蒸馏目标。在Inter-Reliability层面，利用预训练视频奖励分数的指数重新缩放每个回滚的损失；在Intra-Perplexity层面，反向传播同一奖励模型提取逐像素梯度显著性，分解为空间和时间权重，将优化压力集中在最需要改进的区域和帧上；并引入自适应平衡机制防止单一质量维度主导。

Result: 在标准流式视频生成基准上，Stream-R1在视觉质量、运动质量和文本对齐三个维度上均获得一致改进，且无需架构修改或额外推理成本。

Conclusion: 通过奖励感知的自适应重加权策略，可以有效提升视频扩散模型蒸馏质量，为流式视频生成的实际部署提供了更优的加速方案。

Original Abstract: Distillation-based acceleration has become foundational for making autoregressive streaming video diffusion models practical, with distribution matching distillation (DMD) as the de facto choice. Existing methods, however, train the student to match the teacher's output indiscriminately, treating every rollout, frame, and pixel as equally reliable supervision. We argue that this caps distilled quality, since it overlooks two complementary axes of variance in DMD supervision: Inter-Reliability across student rollouts whose supervision varies in reliability, and Intra-Perplexity across spatial regions and temporal frames that contribute unequally to where quality can still be improved. The objective thus conflates two questions under a uniform weight: whether to learn from each rollout, and where to concentrate optimization within it. To address this, we propose Stream-R1, a Reliability-Perplexity Aware Reward Distillation framework that adaptively reweights the distillation objective at both rollout and spatiotemporal-element levels through a single shared reward-guided mechanism. At the Inter-Reliability level, Stream-R1 rescales each rollout's loss by an exponential of a pretrained video reward score, so that rollouts with reliable supervision dominate optimization. At the Intra-Perplexity level, it back-propagates the same reward model to extract per-pixel gradient saliency, which is factored into spatial and temporal weights that concentrate optimization pressure on regions and frames where refinement yields the largest expected gain. An adaptive balancing mechanism prevents any single quality axis from dominating across visual quality, motion quality, and text alignment. Stream-R1 attains consistent improvements on all three dimensions over distillation baselines on standard streaming video generation benchmarks, without architectural modification or additional inference cost.

中文摘要翻译: 基于蒸馏的加速方法已成为使自回归流式视频扩散模型变得实用的基础，其中分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）是事实上的标准选择。然而，现有方法训练学生模型不加区分地匹配教师模型的输出，将所有回滚、帧和像素视为同等可靠的监督信号。我们认为这限制了蒸馏质量的上限，因为它忽略了DMD监督中两个互补的方差维度：不同学生回滚之间的监督可靠性差异（Inter-Reliability），以及空间区域和时间帧对质量改进贡献的不均衡性（Intra-Perplexity）。因此，优化目标在统一权重下混淆了两个问题：是否从每个回滚中学习，以及在回滚内部集中优化哪些位置。为解决此问题，我们提出Stream-R1，一个可靠性-困惑度感知的奖励蒸馏框架，通过单一的共享奖励引导机制在回滚和时空元素两个层面自适应地重加权蒸馏目标。在Inter-Reliability层面，Stream-R1利用预训练视频奖励分数的指数重新缩放每个回滚的损失，使具有可靠监督的回滚主导优化过程。在Intra-Perplexity层面，它反向传播同一奖励模型以提取逐像素梯度显著性，并将其分解为空间和时间权重，从而将优化压力集中在那些改进能够产生最大预期收益的区域和帧上。自适应平衡机制防止任何单一质量维度在视觉质量、运动质量和文本对齐中占据主导地位。在标准流式视频生成基准上，Stream-R1在三个维度上均比蒸馏基线获得一致改进，且无需架构修改或额外推理成本。

</details>


### [45] [A Deeper Dive into the Irreversibility of PolyProtect: Making Protected Face Templates Harder to Invert](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03857)
*Vedrana Krivokuća Hahn,Jérémy Maceiras,Sébastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文深入分析了生物特征模板保护方法PolyProtect的“不可逆性”属性，提出了一种密钥选择算法以增强模板的不可逆性，并通过实验验证了该算法在平衡不可逆性与准确性方面的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: PolyProtect方法使用多元多项式将人脸嵌入转换为保护模板，但先前工作对模板的不可逆性分析不够深入。本文旨在更系统地分析其不可逆性，并解决不同重叠参数下不可逆性不一致以及容易被数值求解器逆推的问题。

Method: 1. 使用基于余弦距离的数值求解器（而非欧氏距离）进行逆推攻击分析；2. 提出一种“密钥选择算法”，通过优化多项式的系数和指数（密钥）来增强模板的不可逆性，替代纯随机密钥；3. 对嵌入进行归一化预处理以提升PolyProtect域的准确率。

Result: 1. 基于余弦距离的求解器比欧氏距离更容易逆推PolyProtected模板；2. 提出的密钥选择算法能显著提升模板不可逆性，并使不同重叠参数下的不可逆性趋于一致；3. 嵌入元素的取值范围会影响准确率，但归一化预处理可改善这一问题。

Conclusion: 密钥选择算法能有效提升PolyProtect模板的不可逆性，并更好地控制不可逆性与准确性之间的权衡；归一化预处理可消除嵌入取值范围对准确率的影响。

Original Abstract: This work presents a deeper analysis of the "irreversibility" property of PolyProtect, a biometric template protection method initially proposed for securing face embeddings. PolyProtect transforms embeddings into protected templates via multivariate polynomials, whose coefficients and exponents are distinct for each subject enrolled in the face recognition system. A polynomial is applied to consecutive sets of elements from a given embedding, where the amount of overlap between the sets is a tunable parameter. We begin our irreversibility analysis by demonstrating that PolyProtected templates are easier to invert using a numerical solver based on cosine distance, as opposed to Euclidean distance (used in the earlier PolyProtect work). To make this inversion more difficult, we then propose a "key selection algorithm", which tries to choose "keys" (coefficients and exponents of the PolyProtect polynomial) that enhance the irreversibility of PolyProtected templates, compared to when the keys are purely random. Our experiments show that this algorithm is effective at generating PolyProtected templates that are significantly more difficult to invert, and that it approximately equalises the irreversibility of PolyProtected templates generated using different "overlap" parameters. This allows for better control of the irreversibility versus accuracy trade-off, known to exist across different overlaps. We also show that accuracy in the PolyProtected domain can be affected by the range in which the embedding elements lie, but that this can be improved by normalizing the embeddings prior to applying PolyProtect. This work is reproducible using our open-source code.

中文摘要翻译: 本文对PolyProtect——一种最初为保护人脸嵌入而提出的生物特征模板保护方法——的“不可逆性”属性进行了更深入的分析。PolyProtect通过多元多项式将嵌入转换为受保护模板，多项式的系数和指数对于人脸识别系统中注册的每个主体都是不同的。多项式应用于给定嵌入中连续的元素集合，集合之间的重叠量是一个可调参数。我们首先通过实验证明，使用基于余弦距离（而非早期PolyProtect工作中使用的欧氏距离）的数值求解器更容易逆推PolyProtected模板。为增加逆推难度，我们随后提出了一种“密钥选择算法”，该算法尝试选择能够增强PolyProtected模板不可逆性的“密钥”（多项式的系数和指数），相比纯随机密钥方案有显著提升。实验表明，该算法能有效生成更难逆推的PolyProtected模板，并且能近似均衡使用不同“重叠”参数生成的PolyProtected模板的不可逆性。这使得我们能够更好地控制不同重叠参数下已知的不可逆性与准确性之间的权衡。我们还证明，嵌入元素的取值范围会影响PolyProtect域的准确率，但通过在应用PolyProtect之前对嵌入进行归一化可以改善这一问题。本工作可通过我们的开源代码复现。

</details>


### [46] [DMGD: Train-Free Dataset Distillation with Semantic-Distribution Matching in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03877)
*Qichao Wang,Yunhong Lu,Hengyuan Cao,Junyi Zhang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需微调的训练自由扩散模型框架DMGD，通过语义匹配和分布匹配的双重引导机制，在数据集蒸馏任务上超越需要额外微调的SOTA方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的数据集蒸馏方法需要额外的微调阶段，且缺乏有效的引导机制，导致效率低下。

Method: 提出DMGD框架，包含：(1) 基于条件似然优化的语义匹配，无需辅助分类器；(2) 动态引导机制增强合成数据多样性；(3) 基于最优传输的分布匹配对齐目标分布结构；(4) 两种高效策略：分布近似匹配和贪心渐进匹配。

Result: 在ImageNet-Woof、ImageNet-Nette和ImageNet-1K上，无需微调即分别超越需要额外微调的SOTA方法2.1%、5.4%和2.4%。

Conclusion: 所提出的训练自由扩散引导框架DMGD有效解决了现有方法需额外微调和引导不足的问题，实现了更高效且性能更优的数据集蒸馏。

Original Abstract: Dataset distillation enables efficient training by distilling the information of large-scale datasets into significantly smaller synthetic datasets. Diffusion based paradigms have emerged in recent years, offering novel perspectives for dataset distillation. However, they typically necessitate additional fine-tuning stages, and effective guidance mechanisms remain underexplored. To address these limitations, we rethink diffusion based dataset distillation and propose a Dual Matching Guided Diffusion (DMGD) framework, centered on efficient training-free guidance. We first establish Semantic Matching via conditional likelihood optimization, eliminating the need for auxiliary classifiers. Furthermore, we propose a dynamic guidance mechanism that enhances the diversity of synthetic data while maintaining semantic alignment. Simultaneously, we introduce an optimal transport (OT) based Distribution Matching approach to further align with the target distribution structure. To ensure efficiency, we develop two enhanced strategies for diffusion based framework: Distribution Approximate Matching and Greedy Progressive Matching. These strategies enable effective distribution matching guidance with minimal computational overhead. Experimental results on ImageNet-Woof, ImageNet-Nette, and ImageNet-1K demonstrate that our training-free approach achieves significant improvements, outperforming state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods requiring additional fine-tuning by average accuracy gains of 2.1%, 5.4%, and 2.4%, respectively.

中文摘要翻译: 数据集蒸馏通过将大规模数据集的信息蒸馏到显著更小的合成数据集中，从而实现高效训练。近年来，基于扩散的范式涌现出来，为数据集蒸馏提供了新的视角。然而，这些方法通常需要额外的微调阶段，且有效的引导机制仍未被充分探索。为了解决这些限制，我们重新思考了基于扩散的数据集蒸馏，并提出了一种以高效训练自由引导为核心的双重匹配引导扩散（DMGD）框架。我们首先通过条件似然优化建立语义匹配，消除了对辅助分类器的需求。此外，我们提出了一种动态引导机制，在保持语义对齐的同时增强合成数据的多样性。同时，我们引入了一种基于最优传输（OT）的分布匹配方法，以进一步与目标分布结构对齐。为确保效率，我们为基于扩散的框架开发了两种增强策略：分布近似匹配和贪心渐进匹配。这些策略能够以最小的计算开销实现有效的分布匹配引导。在ImageNet-Woof、ImageNet-Nette和ImageNet-1K上的实验结果表明，我们的训练自由方法取得了显著改进，平均准确率分别超过需要额外微调的最先进（SOTA）方法2.1%、5.4%和2.4%。

</details>


### [47] [Raising the Ceiling: Better Empirical Fixation Densities for Saliency Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03885)
*Susmit Agrawal,Jannis Hollman,Matthias Kümmerer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于自适应带宽KDE和混合模型的注视点密度估计方法，显著提升了注视点密度图的质量，揭示了当前显著图模型仍有改进空间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前注视点密度估计仍使用固定带宽各向同性高斯KDE，该方法已数十年未变。随着领域转向样本级评估（如失败案例分析、逆基准测试、逐图像模型比较），可靠的逐图像密度估计变得至关重要，现有方法已无法满足需求。

Method: 提出一种原则性混合模型，结合了：(1) 基于Abramson方法的自适应带宽KDE；(2) 中心偏差和均匀成分；(3) 最先进的显著图模型。通过留一被试者交叉验证优化所有参数，以捕捉不同空间和语义类型的观察者间一致性。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，中位逐图像对数似然提升5-15%，AUC提升高达2个百分点；对失败案例分析最相关的图像，改进幅度超过25%。

Conclusion: 经验性注视点密度不应被视为固定的真实值，而是随着方法改进而不断演化的估计值。通过改进的密度估计发现，当前最先进的显著图模型仍有显著改进空间。

Original Abstract: Empirical fixation densities, spatial distributions estimated from human eye-tracking data, are foundational to saliency benchmarking. They directly shape benchmark conclusions, leaderboard rankings, failure case analyses, and scientific claims about human visual behavior. Yet the standard estimation method, fixed-bandwidth isotropic Gaussian KDE, has gone essentially unchanged for decades. This matters now more than ever: as the field shifts toward sample-level evaluation (failure case analysis, inverse benchmarking, per-image model comparison), reliable per-image density estimates become critical. We propose a principled mixture model that combines an adaptive-bandwidth KDE based on Abramson's method, center bias and uniform components, and a state-of-the-art saliency model, to capture different spatial and semantic types of interobserver consistency, and optimize all parameters per image via leave-one-subject-out cross-validation. Our method yields substantially higher interobserver consistency estimates across multiple benchmarks, with median per-image gains of 5-15% in log-likelihood and up to 2 percentage points in AUC. For the most affected images -- precisely those most relevant to failure case analysis -- improvements exceed 25%. We leverage these improved estimates to identify and analyze remaining failure cases of state-of-the-art saliency models, demonstrating that significant headroom for model improvement remains. More broadly, our findings highlight that empirical fixation densities should not be treated as fixed ground truths but as evolving estimates that improve with better methodology.

中文摘要翻译: 经验注视点密度——从人类眼动数据中估计的空间分布——是显著性基准测试的基础。它们直接塑造了基准结论、排行榜排名、失败案例分析以及关于人类视觉行为的科学论断。然而，标准的估计方法——固定带宽各向同性高斯核密度估计（KDE）——已数十年未发生实质性变化。这一问题在当前尤为重要：随着该领域转向样本级评估（失败案例分析、逆基准测试、逐图像模型比较），可靠的逐图像密度估计变得至关重要。我们提出一种原则性的混合模型，它结合了基于Abramson方法的自适应带宽KDE、中心偏差和均匀成分，以及最先进的显著性模型，以捕捉不同空间和语义类型的观察者间一致性，并通过留一被试者交叉验证逐图像优化所有参数。我们的方法在多个基准测试上获得了显著更高的观察者间一致性估计，中位逐图像对数似然提升5-15%，AUC提升高达2个百分点。对于那些受影响最大的图像——恰恰是与失败案例分析最相关的图像——改进幅度超过25%。我们利用这些改进的估计来识别和分析当前最先进显著性模型仍存在的失败案例，表明模型改进仍有显著空间。更广泛地说，我们的发现强调：经验注视点密度不应被视为固定的真实值，而应视为随方法改进而不断演化的估计值。

</details>


### [48] [StateVLM: A State-Aware Vision-Language Model for Robotic Affordance Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03927)
*Xiaowen Sun,Matthias Kerzel,Mengdi Li,Xufeng Zhao,Paul Striker,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为StateVLM的视觉-语言模型，通过辅助回归损失（ARL）训练策略增强VLMs在物体检测和状态定位中的数值推理能力，并构建了OSAR基准数据集。实验表明，ARL能有效提升模型性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在机器人任务中表现出色，但继承了大语言模型（LLMs）的固有限制——在数值推理方面存在困难，特别是在物体检测和物体状态定位任务中表现不佳。为探索VLMs中数值推理作为回归任务的可能性，作者提出了一种新的训练策略。

Method: 提出辅助回归损失（ARL）训练策略：在微调阶段利用box解码器输出计算辅助回归损失，同时在推理阶段保持标准序列预测不变。基于此策略开发了StateVLM模型，用于感知和学习细粒度物体表示，包括精确的物体及其状态定位以及可抓取区域。此外，构建了包含1,172个场景、7,746个物体的OSAR基准数据集。

Result: 在RefCOCO、RefCOCO+、RefCOCOg基准上，ARL使模型性能平均提升1.6%；在OSAR基准上，StateVLM结合ARL比无ARL的模型平均提升5.2%；在OSAR的复杂可供性推理任务中，ARL增强了模型输出的一致性。

Conclusion: ARL训练策略能有效提升VLMs在物体检测、状态定位和可供性推理等数值推理任务中的性能，且不影响推理时的标准序列预测过程。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown remarkable performance in various robotic tasks, as they can perceive visual information and understand natural language instructions. However, when applied to robotics, VLMs remain subject to a fundamental limitation inherent in large language models (LLMs): they struggle with numerical reasoning, particularly in object detection and object-state localization. To explore numerical reasoning as a regression task in VLMs, we propose a novel training strategy to adapt VLMs for object detection and object-state localization. This approach leverages box decoder outputs to compute an Auxiliary Regression Loss (ARL) during fine-tuning, while preserving standard sequence prediction at inference. We leverage this training strategy to develop StateVLM (State-aware Vision-Language Model), a novel model designed to perceive and learn fine-grained object representations, including precise localization of objects and their states, as well as graspable regions. Due to the lack of a benchmark for object-state affordance reasoning, we introduce an open-source benchmark, Object State Affordance Reasoning (OSAR), which contains 1,172 scenes with 7,746 individual objects and corresponding bounding boxes. Comparative experiments on adapted benchmarks (RefCOCO, RefCOCO+, and \mbox{RefCOCOg}) demonstrate that ARL improves model performance by an average of 1.6\% compared to models without ARL. Experiments on the OSAR benchmark further support this finding, showing that StateVLM with ARL achieves an average of 5.2\% higher performance than models without ARL. In particular, ARL is also important for the complex task of affordance reasoning in OSAR, where it enhances the consistency of model outputs.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在各种机器人任务中表现出色，因为它们能够感知视觉信息并理解自然语言指令。然而，当应用于机器人领域时，VLMs仍然存在大语言模型（LLMs）固有的一种根本性局限：它们在数值推理方面存在困难，特别是在物体检测和物体状态定位任务中。为了探索VLMs中作为回归任务的数值推理，我们提出了一种新颖的训练策略，使VLMs适应物体检测和物体状态定位任务。该方法在微调过程中利用框解码器的输出来计算辅助回归损失（ARL），同时在推理阶段保持标准的序列预测。我们利用这种训练策略开发了StateVLM（状态感知视觉-语言模型），这是一种新颖的模型，旨在感知和学习细粒度的物体表示，包括物体及其状态的精确定位，以及可抓取区域。由于缺乏物体状态可供性推理的基准数据集，我们引入了一个开源基准——物体状态可供性推理（OSAR），该基准包含1,172个场景、7,746个独立物体及其对应的边界框。在适配后的基准（RefCOCO、RefCOCO+和RefCOCOg）上的对比实验表明，与未使用ARL的模型相比，ARL将模型性能平均提升了1.6%。在OSAR基准上的实验进一步支持了这一发现，表明使用ARL的StateVLM比未使用ARL的模型平均性能高出5.2%。特别是，ARL对于OSAR中复杂的可供性推理任务也至关重要，它增强了模型输出的一致性。

</details>


### [49] [A Benchmark for Interactive World Models with a Unified Action Generation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03941)
*Jianjie Fang,Yingshan Lei,Qin Wan,Ziyou Wang,Yuchao Huang,Yongyan Xu,Baining Zhao,Weichen Zhang,Chen Gao,Xinlei Chen,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出iWorld-Bench，一个用于评估世界模型物理交互能力的大规模统一基准，包含33万视频片段和6类任务，对14个代表性模型进行了系统评估。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前研究缺乏大规模数据集和统一基准来评估世界模型在物理交互（如距离感知、记忆）方面的能力。

Method: 构建包含33万视频片段的多视角、多天气、多场景数据集，筛选2100个高质量样本；引入动作生成框架统一不同交互模态的评估，设计6类任务（共4900个测试样本），涵盖视觉生成、轨迹跟踪和记忆。

Result: 在14个代表性世界模型上进行评估，识别出关键局限性，为未来研究提供见解。

Conclusion: iWorld-Bench有效评估了世界模型的交互能力，揭示了当前模型的不足，并公开了排行榜以推动后续研究。

Original Abstract: Achieving Artificial General Intelligence (AGI) requires agents that learn and interact adaptively, with interactive world models providing scalable environments for perception, reasoning, and action. Yet current research still lacks large-scale datasets and unified benchmarks to evaluate their physical interaction capabilities. To address this, we propose iWorld-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark for training and testing world models on interaction-related abilities such as distance perception and memory. We construct a diverse dataset with 330k video clips and select 2.1k high-quality samples covering varied perspectives, weather, and scenes. As existing world models differ in interaction modalities, we introduce an Action Generation Framework to unify evaluation and design six task types, generating 4.9k test samples. These tasks jointly assess model performance across visual generation, trajectory following, and memory. Evaluating 14 representative world models, we identify key limitations and provide insights for future research. The iWorld-Bench model leaderboard is publicly available at iWorld-Bench.com.

中文摘要翻译: 实现人工通用智能（AGI）需要能够自适应学习和交互的智能体，而交互式世界模型为感知、推理和行动提供了可扩展的环境。然而，当前研究仍缺乏大规模数据集和统一基准来评估其物理交互能力。为解决这一问题，我们提出了iWorld-Bench，一个用于训练和测试世界模型在距离感知、记忆等交互相关能力上的综合基准。我们构建了一个包含33万个视频片段的多样化数据集，并从中筛选出2100个涵盖不同视角、天气和场景的高质量样本。由于现有世界模型的交互模态各不相同，我们引入了动作生成框架（Action Generation Framework）以统一评估，并设计了六类任务，生成了4900个测试样本。这些任务联合评估了模型在视觉生成、轨迹跟踪和记忆方面的性能。通过对14个代表性世界模型的评估，我们识别了关键局限性，并为未来研究提供了见解。iWorld-Bench模型排行榜已在iWorld-Bench.com公开。

</details>


### [50] [Reservoir property image slices from the Groningen gas field for image translation and segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03942)
*Abdulrahman Al-Fakih,Nabil Sariah,Ardiansyah Koeshidayatullah,SanLinn I. Kaka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于Groningen静态地质模型的高分辨率储层属性图像数据集，包含相、孔隙度、渗透率和含水饱和度的二维PNG图像，并提供了可复现的处理工作流，用于支持地质图像分析方法的基准测试和跨域关系研究。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的公开地质图像数据集有限，不足以支持基于图像和机器学习/深度学习方法在储层表征中的可重复基准测试。

Method: 从Groningen三维静态地质模型中提取并生成对齐的二维PNG图像（相、孔隙度、渗透率、含水饱和度），并开发可复现的软件工作流，支持数据增强、掩码生成、配对图像构建及基线实验。

Result: 生成了一个高分辨率储层属性图像语料库，并提供了完整的可复现处理工作流和示例基线实验结果。

Conclusion: 通过将固定图像数据集与可复现的处理工作流分离，为地球科学、储层建模和机器学习应用提供了透明且可复用的基础资源。

Original Abstract: Reservoir characterization workflows increasingly rely on image-based and machine-learning/deep learning or even generative AI approaches, but openly available geological image datasets suitable for reproducible benchmarking remain limited. Here we describe a high-resolution dataset of reservoir-property image slices derived from the Groningen static geological model. The dataset contains aligned two-dimensional PNG images representing facies, porosity, permeability, and water saturation, generated from three-dimensional reservoir grids and prepared for downstream visualization, segmentation, and image-to-image translation tasks. In addition to the deposited original image corpus, we provide an archived software workflow for reproducing augmentation, mask generation, paired-image construction, and example baseline experiments. The resource is designed to support benchmarking of geological image analysis methods and the study of cross-domain relationships among reservoir properties. By separating the fixed image dataset from the reproducible processing workflow, this work provides a transparent foundation for reuse in geoscience, reservoir modeling, and machine-learning applications.

中文摘要翻译: 储层表征工作流越来越依赖基于图像和机器学习/深度学习甚至生成式人工智能方法，但适用于可重复基准测试的公开地质图像数据集仍然有限。本文描述了一个来源于Groningen静态地质模型的高分辨率储层属性图像切片数据集。该数据集包含对齐的二维PNG图像，分别表示相、孔隙度、渗透率和含水饱和度，这些图像从三维储层网格生成，并已准备好用于下游的可视化、分割和图像到图像的翻译任务。除原始图像语料库外，我们还提供了一个存档的软件工作流，用于复现数据增强、掩码生成、配对图像构建及示例基线实验。该资源旨在支持地质图像分析方法的基准测试以及储层属性间跨域关系的研究。通过将固定图像数据集与可复现的处理工作流分离，本工作为地球科学、储层建模和机器学习应用中的复用提供了透明的基础。

</details>


### [51] [UnAC: Adaptive Visual Prompting with Abstraction and Stepwise Checking for Complex Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03950)
*Yifan Wang,Yun Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UnAC（理解、抽象化、检查）多模态提示方法，通过自适应视觉提示、图像抽象化提示和逐步自检机制，增强LMM在复杂多模态任务中的多步推理能力，在MathVista、MM-Vet和MMMU三个基准上取得优异效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前LMM在视觉感知方面已有显著提升，但在需要基于视觉证据进行多步推理的复杂问题上仍不可靠，缺乏对复杂多模态任务的结构化推理能力。

Method: 1）自适应视觉提示策略：让LMM聚焦图像中的显著区域以捕捉精细细节；2）图像抽象化提示：有效提取图像中的关键信息；3）逐步自检机制：通过验证每个分解后的子问题及其答案来改进推理过程。

Result: 在MathVista、MM-Vet和MMMU三个公开基准上进行了广泛实验，结果表明UnAC方法能有效提升LMM在复杂多模态推理任务上的表现。

Conclusion: UnAC通过将复杂任务分解为理解、抽象化和检查三个阶段，显著增强了LMM在多步视觉推理任务中的可靠性。

Original Abstract: Although recent LMMs have become much stronger at visual perception, they remain unreliable on problems that require multi-step reasoning over visual evidence. In this paper, we present UnAC (Understanding, Abstracting, and Checking), a multimodal prompting method that strengthens reasoning for complex multimodal tasks in LMMs (e.g., GPT-4o, Gemini 1.5, and GPT-4V). To improve image understanding and capture fine details, we propose an adaptive visual prompting strategy that enables LMMs to focus on salient regions. We further design an image-abstraction prompt to effectively extract key information from images. In addition, we introduce a gradual self-checking scheme that improves reasoning by verifying each decomposed subquestion and its answer. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks-MathVista, MM-Vet, and MMMU.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管近期的大多模态模型（LMMs）在视觉感知方面已变得更为强大，但在需要基于视觉证据进行多步推理的问题上仍然不可靠。在本文中，我们提出UnAC（理解、抽象化、检查）——一种多模态提示方法，旨在增强LMM（如GPT-4o、Gemini 1.5和GPT-4V）在复杂多模态任务中的推理能力。为了改进图像理解并捕捉精细细节，我们提出了一种自适应视觉提示策略，使LMM能够聚焦于显著区域。我们还设计了一种图像抽象化提示，以有效提取图像中的关键信息。此外，我们引入了一种逐步自检机制，通过验证每个分解后的子问题及其答案来改进推理过程。我们在三个公开基准——MathVista、MM-Vet和MMMU上进行了广泛实验。

</details>


### [52] [Label-Efficient School Detection from Aerial Imagery via Weakly Supervised Pretraining and Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03968)
*Zakarya Elmimouni,Fares Fourati,Mohamed-Slim Alouini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种弱监督框架，利用稀疏位置点和语义分割自动生成标注，通过两阶段训练（自标注预训练+少量人工标注微调）实现低数据场景下的航空图像学校检测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 许多地区官方学校记录过时、不完整或不可用，人工标注成本高且难以大规模扩展，亟需一种在标注稀缺条件下仍能有效进行学校检测的方法。

Method: 方法包含三部分：(1) 自动标注流程：利用稀疏位置点和语义分割生成基础设施掩码，再生成边界框；(2) 第一阶段训练：使用自动标注图像训练检测器，学习学校外观表示；(3) 第二阶段训练：使用少量人工标注的干净数据对预训练模型进行微调。

Result: 在低数据场景下，仅使用50张人工标注图像即可获得有竞争力的检测性能，显著降低了对昂贵人工标注的需求。

Conclusion: 所提框架以最小监督实现了大规模学校检测，为教育和互联网连接普及提供了高效、可扩展的学校地图绘制方案，且所有模型、代码和自动标注数据将开源。

Original Abstract: Accurate school detection is essential for supporting education initiatives, including infrastructure planning and expanding internet connectivity to underserved areas. However, many regions around the world face challenges due to outdated, incomplete, or unavailable official records. Manual mapping efforts, while valuable, are labor-intensive and lack scalability across large geographic areas. To address this, we propose a weakly supervised framework for school detection from aerial imagery that minimizes the need for human annotations while supporting global mapping efforts. Our method is specifically designed for low-data regimes, where manual annotations are extremely scarce. We introduce an automatic labeling pipeline that leverages sparse location points and semantic segmentation to generate infrastructure masks from which we generate bounding boxes. Using these automatically labeled images, we train our detectors on a first training stage to learn a representation of what schools look like, then using a small set of manually labeled images, we fine-tune the previously trained models on this clean dataset. This two stage training pipeline enables large-scale and strong detection in low-data setting of school infrastructure with minimal supervision. Our results demonstrate strong object detection performance, particularly in the low-data regime, where the models achieve promising results using only 50 manually labeled images, significantly reducing the need for costly annotations. This framework supports education and connectivity initiatives worldwide by providing an efficient and extensible approach to mapping schools from space. All models, training code and auto-labeled data will be publicly released to foster future research and real-world impact.

中文摘要翻译: 准确的学校检测对于支持教育计划至关重要，包括基础设施规划和向服务不足地区扩展互联网连接。然而，全球许多地区面临着官方记录过时、不完整或不可用的挑战。人工标注虽然宝贵，但劳动密集且缺乏跨大地理区域的可扩展性。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种弱监督框架，用于从航空图像中检测学校，该框架最大程度减少了对人工标注的需求，同时支持全球地图绘制工作。我们的方法专门针对低数据场景设计，在此类场景中人工标注极为稀缺。我们引入了一个自动标注流程，利用稀疏位置点和语义分割生成基础设施掩码，再从中生成边界框。使用这些自动标注的图像，我们在第一阶段训练检测器以学习学校的外观表示，然后使用少量人工标注图像在此干净数据集上微调先前训练的模型。这种两阶段训练流程使得在最低监督条件下，能够在低数据场景中对学校基础设施进行大规模、高强度的检测。我们的结果展示了强大的目标检测性能，特别是在低数据场景下，模型仅使用50张人工标注图像即可获得有竞争力的结果，大幅减少了对昂贵标注的需求。该框架通过提供一种高效且可扩展的从太空绘制学校地图的方法，支持全球教育和连接计划。所有模型、训练代码和自动标注数据将公开发布，以促进未来研究和现实世界的影响。

</details>


### [53] [Large-Scale High-Quality 3D Gaussian Head Reconstruction from Multi-View Captures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04035)
*Evangelos Ntavelis,Sean Wu,Mohamad Shahbazi,Fabio Maninchedda,Dmitry Kostiaev,Artem Sevastopolsky,Vittorio Megaro,Trevor Phillips,Alejandro Blumentals,Shridhar Ravikumar,Mehak Gupta,Reinhard Knothe,Jeronimo Bayer,Matthias Vestner,Simon Schaefer,Thomas Etterlin,Christian Zimmermann,Mathias Deschler,Peter Kaufmann,Stefan Brugger,Sebastian Martin,Brian Amberg,Tom Runia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HeadsUp提出了一种可扩展的前馈方法，用于从大规模多摄像头 setups 中重建高质量的3D高斯人头模型，采用编码器-解码器架构将输入视图压缩为紧凑的潜在表示，并解码为UV参数化的3D高斯，在超1万人的数据集上达到SOTA，并支持新身份生成和表情动画。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法在从多视图输入重建3D人头时，通常受限于输入视图数量和分辨率，且无法有效扩展到大规模身份。目标是设计一种可扩展、高效且无需测试时优化的前馈重建方法。

Method: 提出编码器-解码器架构：编码器将多视图输入压缩为紧凑的潜在表示；解码器将该表示解码为一组UV参数化的3D高斯，锚定在标准中性人头模板上。UV表示将3D高斯数量与输入图像的数量和分辨率解耦，从而支持高分辨率多视图训练。

Result: 在包含超过10,000个身份的内部数据集上训练和评估，比现有多视图人头数据集大一个数量级。达到SOTA重建质量，无需测试时优化即可泛化到新身份。系统分析了身份数量、视图数量和模型容量对性能的影响，揭示了质量-计算权衡的实用见解。

Conclusion: HeadsUp在大规模多视图人头重建中实现了可扩展性和高质量，其潜在空间还支持生成新3D身份和通过表情混合变形驱动3D人头动画的下游应用。

Original Abstract: We propose HeadsUp, a scalable feed-forward method for reconstructing high-quality 3D Gaussian heads from large-scale multi-camera setups. Our method employs an efficient encoder-decoder architecture that compresses input views into a compact latent representation. This latent representation is then decoded into a set of UV-parameterized 3D Gaussians anchored to a neutral head template. This UV representation decouples the number of 3D Gaussians from the number and resolution of input images, enabling training with many high-resolution input views. We train and evaluate our model on an internal dataset with more than 10,000 subjects, which is an order of magnitude larger than existing multi-view human head datasets. HeadsUp achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction quality and generalizes to novel identities without test-time optimization. We extensively analyze the scaling behavior of our model across identities, views, and model capacity, revealing practical insights for quality-compute trade-offs. Finally, we highlight the strength of our latent space by showcasing two downstream applications: generating novel 3D identities and animating the 3D heads with expression blendshapes.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出HeadsUp，一种可扩展的前馈方法，用于从大规模多摄像头 setup 中重建高质量的3D高斯人头。该方法采用高效的编码器-解码器架构，将输入视图压缩为紧凑的潜在表示。该潜在表示随后被解码为一组UV参数化的3D高斯，锚定在标准中性人头模板上。这种UV表示将3D高斯数量与输入图像的数量和分辨率解耦，从而能够使用大量高分辨率输入视图进行训练。我们在包含超过10,000个身份的内部数据集上训练和评估该模型，该数据集比现有的多视图人头数据集大一个数量级。HeadsUp达到了最先进的重建质量，无需测试时优化即可泛化到新身份。我们广泛分析了模型在身份、视图和模型容量方面的扩展行为，揭示了质量-计算权衡的实用见解。最后，我们通过展示两个下游应用来突出潜在空间的能力：生成新的3D身份以及使用表情混合变形驱动3D人头动画。

</details>


### [54] [Audio-Visual Intelligence in Large Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04045)
*You Qin,Kai Liu,Shengqiong Wu,Kai Wang,Shijian Deng,Yapeng Tian,Junbin Xiao,Yazhou Xing,Yinghao Ma,Bobo Li,Roger Zimmermann,Lei Cui,Furu Wei,Jiebo Luo,Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述首次从大型基础模型的视角系统回顾了音频-视觉智能（AVI）领域，建立了统一的任务分类体系，涵盖理解、生成和交互三大类任务，并梳理了方法论基础、数据集、基准和评估指标，指出了同步、空间推理、可控性和安全性等开放挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管AVI领域进展迅速，但文献分散、任务分类不一致、评估方式异构，缺乏系统性比较和知识整合。为填补这一空白，该综述旨在提供一个统一的框架，作为大规模AVI未来研究的基础参考。

Method: 通过建立统一的AVI任务分类体系（理解、生成、交互），系统梳理方法论基础（模态分词、跨模态融合、自回归与扩散生成、大规模预训练、指令对齐、偏好优化），并整理代表性数据集、基准和评估指标，进行结构化比较。

Result: 提供了AVI领域首个全面的综述，建立了统一的任务分类框架，识别了同步、空间推理、可控性和安全性等关键开放挑战。

Conclusion: 该综述通过整合快速发展的AVI领域为一个连贯的框架，为未来大规模AVI研究提供了基础性参考。

Original Abstract: Audio-Visual Intelligence (AVI) has emerged as a central frontier in artificial intelligence, bridging auditory and visual modalities to enable machines that can perceive, generate, and interact in the multimodal real world. In the era of large foundation models, joint modeling of audio and vision has become increasingly crucial, i.e., not only for understanding but also for controllable generation and reasoning across dynamic, temporally grounded signals. Recent advances, such as Meta MovieGen and Google Veo-3, highlight the growing industrial and academic focus on unified audio-vision architectures that learn from massive multimodal data. However, despite rapid progress, the literature remains fragmented, spanning diverse tasks, inconsistent taxonomies, and heterogeneous evaluation practices that impede systematic comparison and knowledge integration. This survey provides the first comprehensive review of AVI through the lens of large foundation models. We establish a unified taxonomy covering the broad landscape of AVI tasks, ranging from understanding (e.g., speech recognition, sound localization) to generation (e.g., audio-driven video synthesis, video-to-audio) and interaction (e.g., dialogue, embodied, or agentic interfaces). We synthesize methodological foundations, including modality tokenization, cross-modal fusion, autoregressive and diffusion-based generation, large-scale pretraining, instruction alignment, and preference optimization. Furthermore, we curate representative datasets, benchmarks, and evaluation metrics, offering a structured comparison across task families and identifying open challenges in synchronization, spatial reasoning, controllability, and safety. By consolidating this rapidly expanding field into a coherent framework, this survey aims to serve as a foundational reference for future research on large-scale AVI.

中文摘要翻译: 音频-视觉智能（AVI）已成为人工智能的前沿核心领域，它桥接了听觉与视觉模态，使机器能够在多模态的真实世界中感知、生成和交互。在大规模基础模型时代，音频与视觉的联合建模变得日益关键——不仅用于理解，还用于跨动态、基于时间信号的信号的可控生成与推理。最近的进展，如Meta MovieGen和Google Veo-3，凸显了工业界和学术界对从大规模多模态数据中学习的统一音频-视觉架构日益增长的关注。然而，尽管进展迅速，相关文献仍然分散，涵盖多样化的任务、不一致的分类体系以及异构的评估实践，阻碍了系统性比较和知识整合。本综述首次从大型基础模型的角度对AVI进行了全面回顾。我们建立了一个统一的分类体系，涵盖了AVI任务的广阔图景，从理解（如语音识别、声音定位）到生成（如音频驱动的视频合成、视频到音频）再到交互（如对话、具身或智能体接口）。我们综合了方法论基础，包括模态分词、跨模态融合、自回归和基于扩散的生成、大规模预训练、指令对齐和偏好优化。此外，我们整理了代表性的数据集、基准和评估指标，提供了跨任务家族的结构化比较，并指出了同步、空间推理、可控性和安全性方面的开放挑战。通过将这一快速发展的领域整合为一个连贯的框架，本综述旨在为未来大规模AVI研究提供基础性参考。

</details>

### [55] [A Framework for Exploring and Disentangling Intersectional Bias: A Case Study in Fetal Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02942)
*Aya Elgebaly,Joris Fournel,Benjamin Laine Jønch Jurgensen,Kamil Mikolaj,Anders Christensen,Martin Tolsgaard,Claes Ladefoged,Aasa Feragen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结构化框架，用于探索和检测医学AI中的交叉偏倚。以超过9.4万张胎儿超声图像为案例，发现像素间距（PS）——当前采集协议中的次优参数——是性能差异的一致驱动因素，其影响与胎龄（GA）和BMI等患者因素存在混杂效应。研究表明，仅关注数据代表性不足以解释医学AI中的性能差异，需引入采集感知和交互感知的公平性评估。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究常将医学AI偏倚归因于数据代表性不足（如样本不平衡），但在图像类任务（如胎儿超声）中，即使代表性足够，性能差异仍可能出现。这是因为预测精度强烈依赖图像质量，而图像质量受采集条件、操作者经验及患者因素（如BMI）共同影响，这些因素可能与敏感人口特征相关。因此，观察到的性能差异可能是人口学、临床和采集相关因素的综合作用，而非单纯的数据不平衡，可能掩盖交互或混杂效应。

Method: 提出一个结构化框架，包含三个步骤：（1）无监督切片发现（unsupervised slice discovery），用于识别数据中潜在的子群结构；（2）系统化的因子分析（systematic factor-wise analysis），逐一考察各因素对性能的影响；（3）定向交叉评估（targeted intersectional evaluation），分析多个因素之间的交互效应。案例研究中，对超过9.4万张胎儿超声图像进行胎儿体重估计，分析了最先进的深度学习模型和临床标准Hadlock回归公式。

Result: 像素间距（Pixel Spacing, PS）——当前采集协议中被视为次优的参数——成为性能差异的一致驱动因素。更高的PS与选定子群中高达24%的性能提升相关。由于PS常在BMI高或胎龄（GA）低的情况下调整，该效应存在显著的混杂风险。交叉分析表明，PS相关信号的一部分可由GA解释，但PS相关的改进在不同BMI分层中均持续存在。

Conclusion: 医学AI公平性研究不能仅关注数据代表性，还需要引入采集感知（acquisition-aware）和交互感知（interaction-aware）的评估方法。图像质量相关的采集参数（如像素间距）可能与患者临床特征存在复杂交互，从而产生混杂偏倚，必须通过结构化交叉分析才能准确检测和解释。

Original Abstract: Bias in medical AI is often framed as a problem of representation. However, in image-based tasks such as fetal ultrasound, performance disparities can arise even when representation is adequate, because predictive accuracy depends strongly on image quality. Image quality is shaped by acquisition conditions and operator expertise, as well as patient-dependent factors such as maternal body mass index (BMI), all of which may correlate with sensitive demographic features. Consequently, observed disparities may reflect the combined influence of demographic, clinical, and acquisition-related factors rather than data imbalance alone, and may obscure underlying interaction or confounding effects. We propose a structured framework to explore and detect intersectional bias, combining unsupervised slice discovery, systematic factor-wise analysis, and targeted intersectional evaluation. In a case study of over 94{,}000 ultrasound images for fetal weight estimation, we analyze bias in a state-of-the-art deep learning (DL) model and the clinical standard Hadlock, a regression formula using biometric measurements. Pixel spacing (PS) -- a parameter considered suboptimal in current acquisition protocols -- emerged as a consistent driver of performance differences, with higher PS associated with improvements of up to 24\% in selected subgroups for both models. Because PS is often adapted in cases of high BMI or low gestational age (GA), this effect carries a substantial risk of confounding. Our intersectional analysis revealed that part of the PS-associated signal is explained by GA, while PS-related improvements persist across BMI strata, highlighting the importance of acquisition-aware and interaction-aware evaluation in medical AI fairness research.

中文摘要翻译: 医学AI中的偏倚常被框定为代表性不足的问题。然而，在诸如胎儿超声这类基于图像的任务中，即使代表性充分，性能差异仍可能出现，因为预测准确性强烈依赖图像质量。图像质量受采集条件和操作者专业水平以及患者依赖因素（如母体体重指数BMI）的共同影响，所有这些因素都可能与敏感的人口统计学特征相关。因此，观察到的差异可能反映了人口学、临床和采集相关因素的综合影响，而非单纯的数据不平衡，并可能掩盖潜在的交互或混杂效应。我们提出了一个结构化框架来探索和检测交叉偏倚，该框架结合了无监督切片发现、系统化因子分析和定向交叉评估。在一项涵盖超过9.4万张胎儿体重估计超声图像的案例研究中，我们分析了最先进的深度学习模型和临床标准Hadlock（一种使用生物测量值的回归公式）中的偏倚。像素间距——当前采集协议中被视为次优的参数——成为性能差异的一致驱动因素，更高的PS与选定子群中高达24%的性能提升相关。由于PS常在BMI高或胎龄（GA）低的情况下调整，该效应存在显著的混杂风险。我们的交叉分析揭示，PS相关信号的一部分可由GA解释，而PS相关的改进在不同BMI分层中持续存在，这凸显了在医学AI公平性研究中引入采集感知和交互感知评估的重要性。

</details>


### [56] [eOptShrinkQ: Near-Lossless KV Cache Compression Through Optimal Spectral Denoising and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02905)
*Pei-Chun Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出eOptShrinkQ，一种基于随机矩阵理论的KV缓存压缩方法，通过最优奇异值收缩提取共享上下文结构+残差量化，在Llama-3.1-8B和Ministral-8B上以约2.2比特/条目超越TurboQuant的3.0比特性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Transformer注意力头中的KV缓存存在低秩共享上下文与满秩逐token残差的自然分解，但现有量化方法未充分利用该结构；且标量量化假设的各向同性性质在实际KV缓存中不成立，导致需要额外的异常值处理和内积偏差校正。

Method: 两阶段压缩流程：(1) 基于尖峰随机矩阵模型，使用最优奇异值收缩(eOptShrink)自动提取低秩共享结构（通过BBP相变自动选择秩）；(2) 对满足薄壳性质且坐标离域化的残差部分，使用TurboQuant进行近最优近似的逐向量标量量化，通过谱去噪恢复量化所需的各向同性。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B和Ministral-8B上验证：(1) 每头MSE和内积保真度：同等质量下每条目节省近1比特；(2) LongBench(16任务)：~2.2比特/条目优于TurboQuant的3.0比特；(3) 多针检索：2.2比特接近或超过未压缩FP16，谱去噪对检索密集任务起有益正则化作用。

Conclusion: 谱去噪能够消除异常值处理和内积偏差校正的需求，释放比特用于改进重建；随机矩阵理论提供了自动秩选择、近零内积偏差和坐标离域化三个理论保证，使压缩方法具有可解释性和最优性。

Original Abstract: We show that the key-value (KV) cache in transformer attention heads admits a natural decomposition into a low-rank \emph{shared context} component and a full-rank \emph{per-token} residual, well described by the spiked random matrix model. This observation leads to eOptShrinkQ, a two-stage compression pipeline: optimal singular value shrinkage (eOptShrink) automatically extracts the shared structure, and the residual -- which satisfies the \emph{thin shell property} with delocalized coordinates -- is quantized by TurboQuant~\citep{zandieh2025turboquant}, a recently proposed per-vector scalar quantizer with near-optimal distortion guarantees. By restoring the isotropy that scalar quantization assumes, spectral denoising eliminates the need for both outlier handling and dedicated inner product bias correction, freeing those bits for improved reconstruction.
  The theoretical grounding in random matrix theory provides three guarantees: automatic rank selection via the BBP phase transition, provably near-zero inner product bias on the residual, and coordinate delocalization ensuring near-optimal quantization distortion. Experimentally, we validate eOptShrinkQ on Llama-3.1-8B and Ministral-8B across three levels: per-head MSE and inner product fidelity, where eOptShrinkQ saves nearly one bit per entry over TurboQuant at equivalent quality; end-to-end on LongBench (16 tasks), where eOptShrinkQ at $\sim$2.2 bits per entry outperforms TurboQuant at 3.0 bits; and multi-needle retrieval, where eOptShrinkQ at 2.2 bits closely matches or exceeds uncompressed FP16, suggesting that spectral denoising can act as a beneficial regularizer for retrieval-intensive tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 我们证明，Transformer注意力头中的键值（KV）缓存自然地分解为一个低秩的共享上下文成分和一个满秩的逐token残差，这一分解可由尖峰随机矩阵模型很好地描述。基于此观察，我们提出eOptShrinkQ，一个两阶段压缩流程：最优奇异值收缩（eOptShrink）自动提取共享结构，而残差——满足具有离域化坐标的薄壳性质——由TurboQuant（Zandieh等人，2025）量化，这是一种近期提出的、具有近最优失真保证的逐向量标量量化器。通过恢复标量量化所假设的各向同性，谱去噪消除了对异常值处理和专门内积偏差校正的需求，将这些比特释放用于改进重建。基于随机矩阵理论的理论基础提供了三个保证：通过BBP相变实现自动秩选择、残差上可证明的近零内积偏差，以及确保近最优量化失真的坐标离域化。实验上，我们在Llama-3.1-8B和Ministral-8B上从三个层面验证了eOptShrinkQ：每头MSE和内积保真度方面，在同等质量下eOptShrinkQ每条目比TurboQuant节省近一比特；端到端LongBench（16个任务）上，约2.2比特/条目的eOptShrinkQ优于3.0比特的TurboQuant；以及多针检索任务上，2.2比特的eOptShrinkQ紧密匹配或超过未压缩的FP16，这表明谱去噪可以作为检索密集型任务的有益正则化器。

</details>


### [57] [An End-to-End Framework for Building Large Language Models for Software Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02906)
*Jingkai He,Pengfei Chen,Chenghui Wu,Shuang Liang,Ye Li,Gou Tan,Xiadao Wen,Chuanfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OpsLLM是一种面向软件运维领域的大语言模型，通过人工参与的数据筛选、监督微调和领域过程奖励模型，在知识问答和根因分析任务上显著优于现有开源和闭源模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究因数据质量低、知识碎片化以及学习不充分，尚未实现高效且有效的端到端智能运维，亟需探索LLM在软件运维领域的潜力。

Method: 首先引入Human-in-the-Loop机制从大量原始运维数据中筛选高质量数据构建微调数据集；然后进行监督微调得到基座模型；最后在强化学习阶段引入领域过程奖励模型（DPRM）优化模型在根因分析任务上的准确性和可靠性。

Result: 在QA任务上提升0.2%~5.7%，在RCA任务上提升2.7%~70.3%，且表现出强迁移性。

Conclusion: OpsLLM有效学习和对齐了注入的运维领域知识，在多种难度任务上均优于现有开源和闭源LLM，并开源了7B、14B和32B三个版本及15K微调数据集。

Original Abstract: In the field of software operations, Large Language Models (LLMs) have attracted increasing attention. However, existing research has not yet achieved efficient and effective end-to-end intelligent operations due to low-quality data, fragmented knowledge and insufficient learning. To explore the potential of LLMs in software operations, we propose OpsLLM, a domain-specific LLM that supports both knowledge-based question answering (QA) and root cause analysis (RCA). Moreover, we disclose the detailed workflow for building LLMs specifically in the software operations domain. First, a Human-in-the-Loop mechanism is introduced to curate highquality data from a large collection of operational raw data and construct a fine-tuning dataset. Then, based on the data, supervised fine-tuning is conducted to achieve a base model. Furthermore, we introduce a domain process reward model (DPRM) during the reinforcement learning stage to optimize the accuracy and reliability of the fine-tuned model on RCA tasks. Experimental results on the tasks with diverse difficulties demonstrate that OpsLLMs effectively learns and aligns with the operational domain knowledge infused, outperforming existing open-source and closed-source LLMs in accuracy with improvements of 0.2%~5.7% on QA tasks and 2.7% ~70.3% on RCA tasks, while exhibiting strong transferability. Moreover, we will open-source three versions of OpsLLM with 7B, 14B and 32B parameters, along with a 15K fine-tuning dataset.

中文摘要翻译: 在软件运维领域，大语言模型（LLMs）日益受到关注。然而，由于数据质量低、知识碎片化以及学习不充分，现有研究尚未实现高效且有效的端到端智能运维。为探索LLM在软件运维中的潜力，我们提出OpsLLM，一种同时支持基于知识的问答（QA）和根因分析（RCA）的领域专用大语言模型。此外，我们公开了专门在软件运维领域构建LLM的详细工作流程。首先，引入人机协同机制（Human-in-the-Loop），从大量运维原始数据中筛选高质量数据，构建微调数据集。然后，基于该数据进行监督微调，得到基座模型。此外，我们在强化学习阶段引入领域过程奖励模型（DPRM），以优化微调模型在RCA任务上的准确性和可靠性。在多种难度任务上的实验结果表明，OpsLLM有效学习并对齐了注入的运维领域知识，在QA任务上准确率提升0.2%~5.7%，在RCA任务上提升2.7%~70.3%，优于现有开源和闭源LLM，且展现出强迁移性。此外，我们将开源三个版本的OpsLLM（参数量为7B、14B和32B），以及一个15K规模的微调数据集。

</details>


### [58] [On the Invariants of Softmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02907)
*Wonsuk Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文定义了注意力机制中的“能量场”（行中心化的注意力logit），发现其具有两类不变性：基于Softmax代数结构的机制级不变性（行和为零、秩界、谱特征）和跨模型一致的模型级不变性（能量方差离域化）。后者源于“键矩阵非相干性”，并带来低秩约束和训练监控等实际应用。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Softmax注意力机制将每个查询-键交互映射为概率分布，但其底层结构尚不明确。本文旨在揭示该结构中存在的普适不变性质。

Method: 定义“能量场”（行中心化的注意力logit），从代数结构角度推导机制级不变性（零和约束、秩界、谱签名）；通过实验验证跨架构、跨输入长度的模型级不变性（能量方差离域化），并将其归因于“键矩阵非相干性”。

Result: 机制级：每行零和约束、由头维度决定的秩界、以及相应的谱特征。模型级：能量场方差在键位置上呈离域化分布（而非集中于少数位置），该性质源于键矩阵非相干性。秩界将能量场限制在低维子空间；键矩阵非相干性可用于训练监控。

Conclusion: Softmax注意力中的能量场存在两类普适不变性，既有代数结构保证的必然性质，也有跨模型一致的统计规律，对理解注意力机制和监控训练过程具有实际意义。

Original Abstract: Softmax attention maps every query--key interaction into a probability distribution, but the underlying structure remains largely unexplored. We define the \emph{energy field}, the row-centered attention logit, and show that it exhibits invariant properties across models, architectures, and inputs.
  Two classes of invariants emerge. \emph{Mechanism-level} invariants follow from the algebraic structure of softmax attention. They include a per-row zero-sum constraint, a rank bound determined by the head dimension, and spectral signatures that follow from them. \emph{Model-level} regularities are not required by the mechanism, yet hold in every autoregressive language model we test, spanning several architecture families. The energy field distributes its variance over key positions without concentrating at a few. This delocalization traces to a property of the key matrix we call \emph{key incoherence}.
  These invariants have practical consequences. The rank bound confines the energy field to a low-dimensional subspace. Key incoherence yields a per-head training monitor. All results are verified at multiple context lengths and input texts.

中文摘要翻译: Softmax注意力机制将每个查询-键交互映射为概率分布，但其底层结构在很大程度上尚未被探索。我们定义了“能量场”，即行中心化的注意力logit，并证明它在不同模型、架构和输入下具有不变性质。存在两类不变性。“机制级”不变性源于Softmax注意力的代数结构，包括每行零和约束、由头维度确定的秩界，以及由此导出的谱特征。“模型级”规律并非机制所强制要求，但在我们测试的所有自回归语言模型（涵盖多个架构族）中均成立。能量场将其方差分布在键位置上，而非集中在少数几个位置。这种离域化可追溯到键矩阵的一个性质，我们称之为“键非相干性”。这些不变性具有实际意义：秩界将能量场限制在低维子空间中；键非相干性可产生逐头训练监控指标。所有结果均在多种上下文长度和输入文本上得到验证。

</details>


### [59] [Delay, Plateau, or Collapse: Evaluating the Impact of Systematic Verification Error on RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02909)
*Kazuki Egashira,Mark Vero,Jasper Dekoninck,Florian E. Dorner,Robin Staab,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）中，验证器系统性错误（特别是假阳性）对模型性能的影响，发现系统性假阳性会导致模型性能从次优平台到完全崩溃的多种不良行为，且影响程度取决于错误的特定模式而非总体错误率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究大多将RLVR中的验证器错误视为随机独立噪声，认为其只会减缓训练速度而对最终性能影响有限。然而实际验证器（如静态代码检查器）往往存在系统性错误，这可能导致模型从结构错误的奖励信号中学习到不良的持续行为，亟需深入研究这类系统性错误的影响。

Method: 通过在算术任务上进行受控实验，系统性地引入不同类型的验证器错误（系统性假阴性和系统性假阳性），分析不同错误模式对RLVR训练过程和最终性能的影响。

Result: 系统性假阴性的影响类似于随机噪声；系统性假阳性则会导致从次优平台到性能崩溃的多种不良行为。这些结果不取决于总体错误率，而取决于错误的特定模式。

Conclusion: 与先前结论相反，现实中的验证器系统性错误会严重影响RLVR的训练结果，验证器质量不能仅通过样本级错误率来理解，而需要考虑错误的系统性模式。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become a powerful approach for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). While RLVR is designed for tasks with verifiable ground-truth answers, real-world verifiers (e.g., static code checkers) can introduce errors into the reward signal. Prior analyses have largely treated such errors as random and independent across samples, concluding that errors merely slow training with limited effect on final performance.
